     1	"""Principal resolution and authentication (design.md §16.1/§16.2, FR25-27).
     2	
     3	This module is the single differentiation point between the demo API-key
     4	world and IAP-authenticated production, and (Part B) the point where a
     5	credential is bound to a tenant. All server.py routes depend on
     6	`PrincipalResolver.resolve(request) -> Principal`.
     7	
     8	- `Principal`: the resolved caller identity (mode/tenant_id/employee_id/email).
     9	- `DemoKeyResolver`: AUTH_MODE=demo_key. Reproduces the pre-existing
    10	  X-API-Key / api_key check byte-for-byte, except the tenant_id is now
    11	  whichever tenant's key matched (design §16.2 C-42/W-2: "どの鍵と一致した
    12	  かでテナントに束縛される", no key spans multiple tenants).
    13	- `IapResolver`: AUTH_MODE=iap. Verifies the `X-Goog-IAP-JWT-Assertion`
    14	  header with `google.oauth2.id_token.verify_token`, then resolves the
    15	  verified email to a tenant-scoped Principal via the TenantRegistry's
    16	  system_accounts / email_domains and that tenant's Store.get_identity.
    17	"""
    18	
    19	from __future__ import annotations
    20	
    21	import os
    22	import re
    23	import threading
    24	import time
    25	from dataclasses import dataclass
    26	from typing import Any
    27	
    28	try:
    29	    from fastapi import HTTPException, Request, status
    30	except ImportError as exc:  # pragma: no cover
    31	    raise ImportError(
    32	        "knowledge_discovery.auth requires fastapi (see scripts/requirements.txt)"
    33	    ) from exc
    34	
    35	from knowledge_discovery.tenancy import ContextRouter, TenantRegistry
    36	
    37	# Cloud Run IAP audience format: /projects/<PROJECT_NUMBER>/locations/<REGION>/services/<SERVICE>
    38	IAP_AUDIENCE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^/projects/\d+/locations/[a-z0-9-]+/services/[a-z0-9-]+$")
    39	
    40	IAP_CERTS_URL = "https://www.gstatic.com/iap/verify/public_key"
    41	IAP_ISSUER = "https://cloud.google.com/iap"
    42	IAP_CLOCK_SKEW_SECONDS = 30
    43	
    44	# Default cert cache freshness window (Cache-Control max-age overrides this
    45	# when present on the certs response). "取得失敗時は期限内の旧鍵で継続、期限切れは401":
    46	# once a fetch fails, the previous certs keep being served for one more
    47	# window of the same length before the cache is considered truly expired.
    48	DEFAULT_CERT_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 3600
    49	
    50	
    51	@dataclass
    52	class Principal:
    53	    """Resolved caller identity for a single request (design §16.1)."""
    54	
    55	    mode: str  # "demo" | "human" | "system"
    56	    tenant_id: str
    57	    employee_id: str | None = None
    58	    email: str | None = None
    59	
    60	
    61	class PrincipalResolver:
    62	    """Abstract resolver: request -> Principal. Failure must raise HTTPException(401)."""
    63	
    64	    def resolve(self, request: Request) -> Principal:
    65	        raise NotImplementedError
    66	
    67	
    68	def _unauthorized(detail: str) -> HTTPException:
    69	    return HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail=detail)
    70	
    71	
    72	def _forbidden(detail: str) -> HTTPException:
    73	    return HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail=detail)
    74	
    75	
    76	class DemoKeyResolver(PrincipalResolver):
    77	    """AUTH_MODE=demo_key: the key presented determines the tenant (§16.2).
    78	
    79	    Every caller who presents a key registered in the tenant ledger gets
    80	    mode="demo" bound to whichever tenant that key belongs to, with no
    81	    employee_id -- each API's own request payload supplies the acting
    82	    identity (unchanged from the pre-Part-A behavior). There is no key that
    83	    spans multiple tenants and no way to address another tenant from this
    84	    resolver (design §16.2: "全テナント横断の鍵...は置かない").
    85	    """
    86	
    87	    def __init__(self, registry: TenantRegistry) -> None:
    88	        self.registry = registry
    89	
    90	    def resolve(self, request: Request) -> Principal:
    91	        provided_key = request.headers.get("x-api-key") or request.query_params.get("api_key")
    92	        tenant = self.registry.resolve_by_api_key(provided_key) if provided_key else None
    93	        if tenant is None:
    94	            raise _unauthorized(
    95	                "Invalid or missing API key. Provide header 'X-API-Key' or query parameter 'api_key'."
    96	            )
    97	        return Principal(mode="demo", tenant_id=tenant.tenant_id, employee_id=None, email=None)
    98	
    99	
   100	class _CachingCertsRequest:
   101	    """A `google.auth.transport.Request`-compatible callable that caches the
   102	    IAP public key response in-process.
   103	
   104	    While the cached response is within its freshness window, no network
   105	    call is made. Once the window elapses, a fresh fetch is attempted; if
   106	    that fetch fails, the stale response is still served for one further
   107	    window (grace period) before being treated as truly expired -- at which
   108	    point callers see a normal exception, which IapResolver turns into a
   109	    fail-closed 401 (not 503).
   110	    """
   111	
   112	    def __init__(self, default_ttl_seconds: int = DEFAULT_CERT_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS) -> None:
   113	        self._default_ttl = default_ttl_seconds
   114	        self._transport: Any | None = None
   115	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
   116	        self._cached_response: Any | None = None
   117	        self._fetched_at: float = 0.0
   118	        self._ttl: float = default_ttl_seconds
   119	
   120	    def _get_transport(self) -> Any:
   121	        if self._transport is None:
   122	            from google.auth.transport.requests import Request as GoogleAuthRequest
   123	
   124	            self._transport = GoogleAuthRequest()
   125	        return self._transport
   126	
   127	    @staticmethod
   128	    def _max_age_from_headers(headers: Any) -> float | None:
   129	        if headers is None:
   130	            return None
   131	        cache_control = headers.get("Cache-Control") or headers.get("cache-control")
   132	        if not cache_control:
   133	            return None
   134	        match = re.search(r"max-age=(\d+)", cache_control)
   135	        if match:
   136	            return float(match.group(1))
   137	        return None
   138	
   139	    def __call__(self, url: str, method: str = "GET", **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
   140	        now = time.monotonic()
   141	        with self._lock:
   142	            if self._cached_response is not None and (now - self._fetched_at) < self._ttl:
   143	                return self._cached_response
   144	            try:
   145	                response = self._get_transport()(url, method=method, **kwargs)
   146	                if response.status != 200:
   147	                    raise RuntimeError(f"IAP certs endpoint returned HTTP {response.status}")
   148	                self._cached_response = response
   149	                self._fetched_at = now
   150	                self._ttl = self._max_age_from_headers(getattr(response, "headers", None)) or self._default_ttl
   151	                return response
   152	            except Exception:
   153	                # Fetch failed: serve the stale response through one extra
   154	                # grace window (2x ttl total since the last good fetch)
   155	                # before giving up and letting the caller fail-closed.
   156	                if self._cached_response is not None and (now - self._fetched_at) < (self._ttl * 2):
   157	                    return self._cached_response
   158	                raise
   159	
   160	
   161	class IapResolver(PrincipalResolver):
   162	    """AUTH_MODE=iap: verify the IAP-signed JWT and resolve email -> Principal.
   163	
   164	    The tenant is resolved from the TenantRegistry (§16.2): a system_accounts
   165	    match binds mode=system to that account's tenant; otherwise the email
   166	    domain resolves a tenant and that tenant's own Store.get_identity (via
   167	    ContextRouter) resolves the employee_id for mode=human. IAP_ALLOWED_DOMAINS
   168	    / IAP_SYSTEM_ACCOUNTS envs are absorbed into the ledger (no separate
   169	    single-tenant fallback list; not backward compatible with Part A's env vars).
   170	    """
   171	
   172	    def __init__(
   173	        self,
   174	        registry: TenantRegistry,
   175	        router: ContextRouter,
   176	        audience: str,
   177	        cert_cache: _CachingCertsRequest | None = None,
   178	    ) -> None:
   179	        self.registry = registry
   180	        self.router = router
   181	        self.audience = audience
   182	        self._cert_request = cert_cache or _CachingCertsRequest()
   183	
   184	    def resolve(self, request: Request) -> Principal:
   185	        assertion = request.headers.get("x-goog-iap-jwt-assertion")
   186	        if not assertion:
   187	            raise _unauthorized("Missing X-Goog-IAP-JWT-Assertion header.")
   188	
   189	        try:
   190	            from google.oauth2 import id_token as google_id_token
   191	        except ImportError as exc:  # pragma: no cover
   192	            raise RuntimeError("google-auth is required for AUTH_MODE=iap (see scripts/requirements.txt)") from exc
   193	
   194	        try:
   195	            payload = google_id_token.verify_token(
   196	                assertion,
   197	                request=self._cert_request,
   198	                audience=self.audience,
   199	                certs_url=IAP_CERTS_URL,
   200	                clock_skew_in_seconds=IAP_CLOCK_SKEW_SECONDS,
   201	            )
   202	        except Exception as exc:
   203	            raise _unauthorized(f"Invalid IAP assertion: {exc}") from exc
   204	
   205	        if payload.get("iss") != IAP_ISSUER:
   206	            raise _unauthorized("IAP assertion has an unexpected issuer.")
   207	
   208	        email = payload.get("email")
   209	        if not email:
   210	            raise _unauthorized("IAP assertion is missing the required 'email' claim.")
   211	        email = str(email).strip().lower()
   212	
   213	        system_tenant = self.registry.resolve_by_system_account(email)
   214	        if system_tenant is not None:
   215	            return Principal(mode="system", tenant_id=system_tenant.tenant_id, employee_id=None, email=email)
   216	
   217	        domain = email.rsplit("@", 1)[-1] if "@" in email else ""
   218	        tenant = self.registry.resolve_by_email_domain(domain)
   219	        if tenant is None:
   220	            raise _forbidden("Email domain is not registered for any tenant.")
   221	
   222	        tenant_store = self.router.for_tenant(tenant.tenant_id).store
   223	        employee_id = tenant_store.get_identity(email)
   224	        if employee_id is None:
   225	            raise _forbidden("No employee identity is registered for this email.")
   226	
   227	        return Principal(mode="human", tenant_id=tenant.tenant_id, employee_id=employee_id, email=email)
   228	
   229	
   230	def validate_iap_audience_format(audience: str) -> None:
   231	    """Startup-time format check, called only when AUTH_MODE=iap (§16.1)."""
   232	    if not IAP_AUDIENCE_PATTERN.match(audience or ""):
   233	        raise RuntimeError(
   234	            "IAP_AUDIENCE must match '/projects/<PROJECT_NUMBER>/locations/<REGION>/services/<SERVICE>' "
   235	            f"when AUTH_MODE=iap (got: {audience!r})"
   236	        )
   237	
   238	
   239	def build_principal_resolver(
   240	    registry: TenantRegistry,
   241	    router: ContextRouter,
   242	    auth_mode: str | None = None,
   243	) -> PrincipalResolver:
   244	    """Build the configured PrincipalResolver from AUTH_MODE / IAP_AUDIENCE env vars.
   245	
   246	    The tenant ledger (registry) is the single source of truth for API keys,
   247	    email domains and system_accounts in both modes (§16.2); there is no
   248	    per-call tenant_id parameter to keep in sync with it.
   249	    """
   250	    mode = auth_mode or os.environ.get("AUTH_MODE", "demo_key")
   251	    if mode == "iap":
   252	        audience = os.environ.get("IAP_AUDIENCE", "")
   253	        validate_iap_audience_format(audience)
   254	        return IapResolver(registry=registry, router=router, audience=audience)
   255	    return DemoKeyResolver(registry=registry)

    52	class HttpSession(Protocol):
    53	    """The subset of `AuthorizedSession` / `requests.Session` this module needs."""
    54	
    55	    def get(self, url: str, params: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> Any: ...
    56	
    57	
    58	def default_session(scopes: tuple[str, ...] = DEFAULT_SCOPES) -> HttpSession:
    59	    """Build a real `AuthorizedSession` from `google.auth.default()` (author ADC).
    60	
    61	    Imports `google.auth` lazily so importing this module (e.g. for
    62	    `SourceConnector` typing) never requires ADC to be configured — only
    63	    calling this function does.
    64	    """
    65	    import google.auth
    66	    from google.auth.transport.requests import AuthorizedSession
    67	
    68	    credentials, _ = google.auth.default(scopes=list(scopes))
    69	    return AuthorizedSession(credentials)

    72	def _rfc3339_to_utc_date(value: str) -> str | None:
    73	    try:
    74	        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    75	    except (ValueError, AttributeError, TypeError):
    76	        return None
    77	    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    78	        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    79	    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()
    80	
    81	
    82	def _rfc3339_to_utc_datetime(value: str) -> datetime | None:
    83	    try:
    84	        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    85	    except (ValueError, AttributeError, TypeError):
    86	        return None
    87	    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    88	        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    89	    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)

    91	
    92	def _b64url_decode(data: str) -> str:
    93	    padded = data + "=" * (-len(data) % 4)
    94	    try:
    95	        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(padded).decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    96	    except (ValueError, TypeError):
    97	        return ""
    98	
    99	
   100	def _extract_plain_text(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   101	    """Depth-first search for a `text/plain` MIME part; empty string if none."""
   102	    mime_type = payload.get("mimeType", "")
   103	    body = payload.get("body", {}) or {}
   104	    if mime_type == "text/plain" and body.get("data"):
   105	        return _b64url_decode(body["data"])
   106	    for part in payload.get("parts", []) or []:
   107	        text = _extract_plain_text(part)
   108	        if text:
   109	            return text
   110	    return ""
   111	
   112	
   113	def _internal_date_to_iso(value: Any) -> str:
   114	    try:
   115	        millis = int(value)
   116	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   117	        return ""
   118	    return datetime.fromtimestamp(millis / 1000, tz=timezone.utc).isoformat()

   121	@dataclass
   122	class _PageResult:
   123	    items: list[dict[str, Any]]
   124	    complete: bool
   125	
   126	
   127	class GoogleWorkspaceConnector(SourceConnector):
   128	    """Tasks / Calendar / Gmail connector, driven by an injected HTTP session."""
   129	
   130	    def __init__(self, session: HttpSession) -> None:
   131	        self.session = session
   132	
   133	    # -- SourceConnector -----------------------------------------------
   134	
   135	    def fetch(self, owner_employee_id: str, today: str) -> FetchResult:
   136	        errors: list[str] = []
   137	
   138	        tasks, tasks_complete = self._fetch_tasks(errors)
   139	        schedules, cancelled_ids, calendar_complete = self._fetch_calendar(today, errors)
   140	        mails, gmail_complete = self._fetch_gmail(errors)
   141	
   142	        complete = tasks_complete and calendar_complete and gmail_complete
   143	        logger.info(
   144	            "gws_connector: owner=%s tasks=%d schedules=%d cancelled=%d mails=%d "
   145	            "complete=%s error_count=%d",
   146	            owner_employee_id,
   147	            len(tasks),
   148	            len(schedules),
   149	            len(cancelled_ids),
   150	            len(mails),
   151	            complete,
   152	            len(errors),
   153	        )
   154	        return FetchResult(
   155	            tasks=tasks,
   156	            schedules=schedules,
   157	            mails=mails,
   158	            complete=complete,
   159	            errors=errors,
   160	            cancelled_ids=cancelled_ids,
   161	        )
   162	
   163	    # -- generic paging --------------------------------------------------
   164	
   165	    def _paginate(
   166	        self,
   167	        url: str,
   168	        params: dict[str, Any],
   169	        items_key: str,
   170	        context: str,
   171	        errors: list[str],
   172	    ) -> _PageResult:
   173	        items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   174	        page_token: str | None = None
   175	        while True:
   176	            request_params = dict(params)
   177	            if page_token:
   178	                request_params["pageToken"] = page_token
   179	            try:
   180	                response = self.session.get(url, params=request_params)
   181	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - network/transport errors vary by client
   182	                errors.append(f"{context}: request failed ({type(exc).__name__})")
   183	                return _PageResult(items, False)
   184	            status_code = getattr(response, "status_code", None)
   185	            if status_code != 200:
   186	                errors.append(f"{context}: HTTP {status_code}")
   187	                return _PageResult(items, False)
   188	            try:
   189	                data = response.json()
   190	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - malformed body from a real API
   191	                errors.append(f"{context}: invalid JSON response")
   192	                return _PageResult(items, False)
   193	            items.extend(data.get(items_key) or [])
   194	            page_token = data.get("nextPageToken")
   195	            if not page_token:
   196	                return _PageResult(items, True)
   197	
   198	    # -- Tasks -------------------------------------------------------------
   199	
   200	    def _fetch_tasks(self, errors: list[str]) -> tuple[list[TaskRecord], bool]:
   201	        lists_result = self._paginate(
   202	            f"{TASKS_BASE}/users/@me/lists", {}, "items", "tasks.lists", errors
   203	        )
   204	        if not lists_result.complete:
   205	            return [], False
   206	
   207	        records: list[TaskRecord] = []
   208	        complete = True
   209	        for task_list in lists_result.items:
   210	            list_id = task_list.get("id")
   211	            if not list_id:
   212	                continue
   213	            tasks_result = self._paginate(
   214	                f"{TASKS_BASE}/lists/{list_id}/tasks",
   215	                {"showCompleted": "true", "showHidden": "true", "maxResults": 100},
   216	                "items",
   217	                f"tasks.list[{list_id}]",
   218	                errors,
   219	            )
   220	            if not tasks_result.complete:
   221	                complete = False
   222	            for raw in tasks_result.items:
   223	                records.append(self._map_task(list_id, raw))
   224	        return records, complete
   225	
   226	    def _map_task(self, list_id: str, raw: dict[str, Any]) -> TaskRecord:
   227	        due = raw.get("due")
   228	        return TaskRecord(
   229	            source_id=f"gws_task_{list_id}_{raw.get('id', '')}",
   230	            title=raw.get("title", ""),
   231	            description=raw.get("notes", ""),
   232	            due_date=_rfc3339_to_utc_date(due) if due else None,
   233	            status="done" if raw.get("status") == "completed" else "todo",
   234	            last_updated_at=raw.get("updated", ""),
   235	        )
   236	
   237	    # -- Calendar ------------------------------------------------------
   238	
   239	    def _fetch_calendar(
   240	        self, today: str, errors: list[str]
   241	    ) -> tuple[list[ScheduleRecord], list[str], bool]:
   242	        days_ahead = int(os.environ.get("GWS_CAL_DAYS_AHEAD", "3"))
   243	        today_date = date.fromisoformat(today)
   244	        yesterday = today_date - timedelta(days=1)
   245	        horizon = today_date + timedelta(days=days_ahead)
   246	        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   247	
   248	        time_min = datetime.combine(yesterday, datetime.min.time(), tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   249	        time_max = datetime.combine(
   250	            horizon + timedelta(days=1), datetime.min.time(), tzinfo=timezone.utc
   251	        )
   252	        params = {
   253	            "singleEvents": "true",
   254	            "orderBy": "startTime",
   255	            "timeMin": time_min.isoformat(),
   256	            "timeMax": time_max.isoformat(),
   257	            "maxResults": 250,
   258	        }
   259	        events_result = self._paginate(
   260	            f"{CALENDAR_BASE}/calendars/primary/events",
   261	            params,
   262	            "items",
   263	            "calendar.events",
   264	            errors,
   265	        )
   266	
   267	        schedules: list[ScheduleRecord] = []
   268	        cancelled_ids: list[str] = []
   269	        for event in events_result.items:
   270	            event_id = event.get("id", "")
   271	            if event.get("status") == "cancelled":
   272	                if event_id:
   273	                    cancelled_ids.append(event_id)
   274	                continue
   275	            record = self._map_calendar_event(event, today_date, yesterday, horizon, now)
   276	            if record is not None:
   277	                schedules.append(record)
   278	        return schedules, cancelled_ids, events_result.complete
   279	
   280	    def _map_calendar_event(
   281	        self,
   282	        event: dict[str, Any],
   283	        today_date: date,
   284	        yesterday: date,
   285	        horizon: date,
   286	        now: datetime,
   287	    ) -> ScheduleRecord | None:
   288	        start = event.get("start", {}) or {}
   289	        end = event.get("end", {}) or {}
   290	        is_all_day = "dateTime" not in start
   291	        start_raw = start.get("dateTime") or start.get("date")
   292	        if not start_raw:
   293	            return None
   294	        start_date_str = start_raw if is_all_day else _rfc3339_to_utc_date(start_raw)
   295	        if start_date_str is None:
   296	            return None
   297	        try:
   298	            event_date = date.fromisoformat(start_date_str)
   299	        except ValueError:
   300	            return None
   301	
   302	        if yesterday <= event_date < today_date:
   303	            kind = "meeting_review"
   304	        elif today_date <= event_date <= horizon:
   305	            kind = "meeting_prep"
   306	            if event_date == today_date and not is_all_day:
   307	                end_raw = end.get("dateTime")
   308	                end_dt = _rfc3339_to_utc_datetime(end_raw) if end_raw else None
   309	                if end_dt is not None and end_dt <= now:
   310	                    kind = "meeting_review"
   311	        else:
   312	            return None
   313	
   314	        event_id = event.get("id", "")
   315	        return ScheduleRecord(
   316	            source_id=f"gws_cal_{event_id}_{kind}",
   317	            kind=kind,
   318	            title=event.get("summary", ""),
   319	            due_date=start_date_str,
   320	        )
   321	
   322	    # -- Gmail (part D) --------------------------------------------------
   323	
   324	    def _fetch_gmail(self, errors: list[str]) -> tuple[list[MailRecord], bool]:
   325	        if os.environ.get("GWS_GMAIL_ENABLED", "false").lower() != "true":
   326	            return [], True
   327	
   328	        label_id, label_ok = self._resolve_gmail_label(errors)
   329	        if not label_ok:
   330	            return [], False
   331	        if label_id is None:
   332	            return [], True  # opt-in label not created yet: zero mails, not an error
   333	
   334	        days = int(os.environ.get("GWS_GMAIL_DAYS", "7"))
   335	        max_results = int(os.environ.get("GWS_GMAIL_MAX_RESULTS", "20"))
   336	        body_chars = int(os.environ.get("GWS_MAIL_BODY_CHARS", "2000"))
   337	
   338	        message_ids, list_complete = self._list_gmail_message_ids(
   339	            label_id, days, max_results, errors
   340	        )
   341	
   342	        records: list[MailRecord] = []
   343	        complete = list_complete
   344	        for msg_id in message_ids:
   345	            try:
   346	                response = self.session.get(
   347	                    f"{GMAIL_BASE}/users/me/messages/{msg_id}", params={"format": "full"}
   348	                )
   349	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   350	                errors.append(f"gmail.messages.get: request failed ({type(exc).__name__})")
   351	                complete = False
   352	                continue
   353	            status_code = getattr(response, "status_code", None)
   354	            if status_code != 200:
   355	                errors.append(f"gmail.messages.get: HTTP {status_code}")
   356	                complete = False
   357	                continue
   358	            try:
   359	                data = response.json()
   360	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
   361	                errors.append("gmail.messages.get: invalid JSON response")
   362	                complete = False
   363	                continue
   364	            records.append(self._map_gmail_message(msg_id, data, body_chars))
   365	
   366	        logger.info("gws_connector: gmail messages fetched=%d complete=%s", len(records), complete)
   367	        return records, complete
   368	
   369	    def _resolve_gmail_label(self, errors: list[str]) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
   370	        try:
   371	            response = self.session.get(f"{GMAIL_BASE}/users/me/labels")
   372	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   373	            errors.append(f"gmail.labels.list: request failed ({type(exc).__name__})")
   374	            return None, False
   375	        status_code = getattr(response, "status_code", None)
   376	        if status_code != 200:
   377	            errors.append(f"gmail.labels.list: HTTP {status_code}")
   378	            return None, False
   379	        try:
   380	            data = response.json()
   381	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
   382	            errors.append("gmail.labels.list: invalid JSON response")
   383	            return None, False
   384	        for label in data.get("labels") or []:
   385	            if label.get("name") == GMAIL_LABEL_NAME:
   386	                return label.get("id"), True
   387	        return None, True
   388	
   389	    def _list_gmail_message_ids(
   390	        self, label_id: str, days: int, max_results: int, errors: list[str]
   391	    ) -> tuple[list[str], bool]:
   392	        ids: list[str] = []
   393	        page_token: str | None = None
   394	        query = f"newer_than:{days}d"
   395	        while len(ids) < max_results:
   396	            params: dict[str, Any] = {
   397	                "labelIds": label_id,
   398	                "q": query,
   399	                "maxResults": min(100, max_results - len(ids)),
   400	            }
   401	            if page_token:
   402	                params["pageToken"] = page_token
   403	            try:
   404	                response = self.session.get(f"{GMAIL_BASE}/users/me/messages", params=params)
   405	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   406	                errors.append(f"gmail.messages.list: request failed ({type(exc).__name__})")
   407	                return ids, False
   408	            status_code = getattr(response, "status_code", None)
   409	            if status_code != 200:
   410	                errors.append(f"gmail.messages.list: HTTP {status_code}")
   411	                return ids, False
   412	            try:
   413	                data = response.json()
   414	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
   415	                errors.append("gmail.messages.list: invalid JSON response")
   416	                return ids, False
   417	            for item in data.get("messages") or []:
   418	                msg_id = item.get("id")
   419	                if msg_id:
   420	                    ids.append(msg_id)
   421	                if len(ids) >= max_results:
   422	                    break
   423	            page_token = data.get("nextPageToken")
   424	            if not page_token:
   425	                break
   426	        return ids[:max_results], True
   427	
   428	    def _map_gmail_message(self, msg_id: str, data: dict[str, Any], body_chars: int) -> MailRecord:
   429	        payload = data.get("payload", {}) or {}
   430	        headers = {
   431	            (h.get("name") or "").lower(): h.get("value", "")
   432	            for h in payload.get("headers") or []
   433	        }
   434	        subject = headers.get("subject", "")[:80]
   435	        body = _extract_plain_text(payload)[:body_chars]
   436	        received_at = _internal_date_to_iso(data.get("internalDate"))
   437	        return MailRecord(
   438	            source_id=f"gws_mail_{msg_id}",
   439	            subject=subject,
   440	            body=body,
   441	            received_at=received_at,
   442	        )

     1	"""FastAPI server for knowledge-discovery (Milestone 2).
     2	
     3	Follows design.md §3 - §7, §16.1, §16.2:
     4	- Every route resolves a Principal (demo/human/system, see auth.py) and is
     5	  gated by the §16.1 permission table. AUTH_MODE=demo_key (default) checks
     6	  X-API-Key/api_key against the tenant ledger; AUTH_MODE=iap verifies the
     7	  IAP-signed assertion instead.
     8	- Every route then looks up `ContextRouter.for_tenant(principal.tenant_id)`
     9	  (§16.2) and operates only on that TenantContext's store/service/secretary --
    10	  there is no code path that reaches another tenant's data.
    11	- POST /api/query: Submit question, run 2-track matching, dispatch asks.
    12	- GET /api/requester/{requester_id}/status: Requester projection (strict candidate ID & consent isolation rules).
    13	- GET /api/candidate/{agent_id}/asks: Candidate inbox for received asks.
    14	- POST /api/candidate/{agent_id}/consent: Submit consent decision (granted / declined + reason & attachment).
    15	- GET /api/audit/messages: Audit view with fail-closed masked payloads and funnel metrics.
    16	- GET /attachments/{id}: Static document delivery (C-24).
    17	- Serves web UI at /requester, /candidate, /audit.
    18	"""

   136	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   137	# Application Factory

   139	
   140	def create_app_from_env() -> Any:
   141	    """Production factory: wire FirestoreStore / Gemini adapters from environment.
   142	
   143	    Env vars:
   144	    - TENANTS_JSON           -> tenant ledger (default: 1 tenant, see tenancy.py)
   145	    - USE_FIRESTORE=1        -> FirestoreStore per tenant, database=<tenant.database> (§16.2)
   146	    - GEMINI_API_KEY set     -> GeminiEmbedder + GeminiConnectionInferencer (shared across tenants)
   147	    - DEMO_API_KEY           -> default tenant's API key when TENANTS_JSON is unset
   148	
   149	    Run with: uvicorn 'knowledge_discovery.server:create_app_from_env' --factory
   150	    """
   151	    registry = TenantRegistry.from_env()
   152	    use_firestore = os.environ.get("USE_FIRESTORE") == "1"
   153	    project = os.environ.get("GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT")
   154	
   155	    def store_factory(tenant: TenantConfig) -> Store:
   156	        if use_firestore:
   157	            from knowledge_discovery.firestore_store import FirestoreStore
   158	
   159	            return FirestoreStore(project=project, database=tenant.database)
   160	        new_store = InMemoryStore()
   161	        from scripts.generate_seeds import populate_store
   162	
   163	        populate_store(new_store, dry_run=False)
   164	        return new_store
   165	
   166	    use_vertex = os.environ.get("GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI", "").lower() in ("1", "true")
   167	    matching_engine: MatchingEngine | None = None
   168	    llm_client: Any | None = None
   169	    if os.environ.get("GEMINI_API_KEY") or use_vertex:
   170	        from knowledge_discovery.gemini_adapters import (
   171	            GeminiConnectionInferencer,
   172	            GeminiEmbedder,
   173	            _build_genai_client,
   174	        )
   175	
   176	        # Gemini/embedding clients are stateless (§16.2), so one instance is
   177	        # shared across every tenant's MatchingEngine; only the Store differs.
   178	        matching_engine = MatchingEngine(
   179	            embedder=GeminiEmbedder(),
   180	            inferencer=GeminiConnectionInferencer(),
   181	            vector_floor=float(os.environ.get("VECTOR_FLOOR", "0.20")),
   182	            connection_threshold=float(os.environ.get("CONNECTION_THRESHOLD", "0.50")),
   183	            max_dispatch_k=3,
   184	            funnel_limit=20,
   185	        )
   186	        # Secretary question drafts and profile-diff extraction use the same
   187	        # Gemini client as connection inference (§14.4/§14.5, V-8/E-7/S-6).
   188	        llm_client = _build_genai_client(os.environ.get("GEMINI_API_KEY", ""))
   189	
   190	    return create_app(
   191	        registry=registry,
   192	        store_factory=store_factory,
   193	        matching_engine=matching_engine,
   194	        llm_client=llm_client,
   195	    )
   196	
   197	
   198	def create_app(
   199	    store: Store | None = None,
   200	    service: KnowledgeDiscoveryService | None = None,
   201	    api_key: str | None = None,
   202	    llm_client: Any | None = None,
   203	    principal_resolver: PrincipalResolver | None = None,
   204	    registry: TenantRegistry | None = None,
   205	    store_factory: Callable[[TenantConfig], Store] | None = None,
   206	    matching_engine: MatchingEngine | None = None,
   207	) -> Any:
   208	    """Create and configure the FastAPI application.
   209	
   210	    Tenancy (design §16.2): every tenant gets its own Store + KnowledgeDiscoveryService
   211	    + SecretaryService, built lazily and cached per-tenant by a ContextRouter; routes
   212	    below resolve `principal.tenant_id` and operate only on that tenant's context.
   213	
   214	    Test-friendly single-tenant compatibility: when `registry` is omitted, a
   215	    single-tenant registry (tenant_id="meridian") is built from `api_key`/
   216	    `DEMO_API_KEY`, and an explicitly passed `store=`/`service=` is used as
   217	    that one tenant's context (current single-tenant behavior, unchanged).
   218	    For a real multi-tenant ledger, pass `registry=` (e.g. TenantRegistry.from_env())
   219	    and `store_factory=` (how to build a fresh Store for a given tenant); `store=`/
   220	    `service=` are ignored in that case.
   221	    """
   222	    if FastAPI is None:
   223	        raise RuntimeError("fastapi is not installed. Please install requirements.txt.")
   224	
   225	    expected_api_key = api_key or os.environ.get("DEMO_API_KEY", DEFAULT_DEMO_API_KEY)
   226	
   227	    preset_store: Store | None = None
   228	    preset_service: KnowledgeDiscoveryService | None = None
   229	    if registry is None:
   230	        registry = TenantRegistry.single(
   231	            tenant_id=DEFAULT_TENANT_ID,
   232	            database=DEFAULT_DATABASE,
   233	            email_domains=list(DEFAULT_EMAIL_DOMAINS),
   234	            api_key=expected_api_key,
   235	        )
   236	        preset_store = store
   237	        preset_service = service
   238	
   239	    def _default_store_factory(_tenant: TenantConfig) -> Store:
   240	        new_store = InMemoryStore()
   241	        from scripts.generate_seeds import populate_store
   242	
   243	        populate_store(new_store, dry_run=False)
   244	        return new_store
   245	
   246	    effective_store_factory = store_factory or _default_store_factory
   247	
   248	    # Gemini/embedding clients are stateless, so one MatchingEngine instance
   249	    # is shared across every tenant's context (§16.2); only the Store differs
   250	    # per tenant.
   251	    shared_matching_engine = matching_engine
   252	    if shared_matching_engine is None and preset_service is not None:
   253	        shared_matching_engine = preset_service.matching_engine
   254	    if shared_matching_engine is None:
   255	        shared_matching_engine = MatchingEngine(
   256	            embedder=DeterministicEmbedder(),
   257	            inferencer=FakeConnectionInferencer(),
   258	            vector_floor=0.20,
   259	            connection_threshold=0.50,
   260	            max_dispatch_k=3,
   261	            funnel_limit=20,
   262	        )
   263	
   264	    def _build_tenant_context(tenant: TenantConfig) -> TenantContext:
   265	        if preset_store is not None and tenant.tenant_id == DEFAULT_TENANT_ID:
   266	            tenant_store = preset_store
   267	        else:
   268	            tenant_store = effective_store_factory(tenant)
   269	
   270	        if preset_service is not None and tenant.tenant_id == DEFAULT_TENANT_ID:
   271	            tenant_service = preset_service
   272	        else:
   273	            tenant_service = KnowledgeDiscoveryService(store=tenant_store, matching_engine=shared_matching_engine)
   274	
   275	        tenant_secretary = SecretaryService(
   276	            store=tenant_store,
   277	            kd_service=tenant_service,
   278	            matching_engine=shared_matching_engine,
   279	            llm_client=llm_client,
   280	            # §16.3 data-source seam: SOURCE_CONNECTOR=seed|google_workspace (default seed).
   281	            # One connector instance per tenant context; SeedConnector is a no-op.
   282	            connector=build_connector_from_env(),
   283	        )
   284	        return TenantContext(
   285	            tenant=tenant,
   286	            store=tenant_store,
   287	            service=tenant_service,
   288	            secretary=tenant_secretary,
   289	            matching=shared_matching_engine,
   290	            static_counts={},
   291	        )
   292	
   293	    router = ContextRouter(registry, context_factory=_build_tenant_context)
   294	
   295	    app = FastAPI(
   296	        title="Knowledge Discovery API",
   297	        description="Tacit knowledge discovery and synergy matching engine (Milestone 2)",
   298	        version="0.2.0",
   299	        # No unauthenticated surface beyond the UI shells (S-2): API schema
   300	        # endpoints are disabled rather than left open on the public demo URL
   301	        docs_url=None,
   302	        redoc_url=None,
   303	        openapi_url=None,
   304	    )
   305	
   306	    web_dir = Path(__file__).parent / "web"
   307	
   308	    # Principal resolution dependency (design §16.1/§16.2): AUTH_MODE=demo_key
   309	    # (default) reproduces the old X-API-Key/api_key check, now resolving the
   310	    # tenant from whichever tenant's key matched; AUTH_MODE=iap verifies the
   311	    # IAP-signed assertion instead. Every route below depends on this single
   312	    # function and then applies the §16.1 permission table.
   313	    resolver = principal_resolver or build_principal_resolver(registry, router)
   314	
   315	    def get_principal(request: Request) -> Principal:
   316	        return resolver.resolve(request)
   317	
   318	    def get_context(principal: Principal = Depends(get_principal)) -> TenantContext:
   319	        """Resolve the caller's own TenantContext (§16.2). This is the only
   320	        place a route obtains a store/service/secretary -- there is no
   321	        function that returns another tenant's context."""
   322	        try:
   323	            return router.for_tenant(principal.tenant_id)
   324	        except KeyError:
   325	            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Unknown tenant.")
   326	
   327	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   328	    # §16.1 permission-table helpers (default-deny: every route below states
   329	    # explicitly what each of demo/human/system may do; nothing is implicit)
   330	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   331	
   332	    def _deny_system(principal: Principal) -> None:
   333	        if principal.mode == "system":
   334	            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Not permitted for system principals.")
   335	
   336	    def _deny_human(principal: Principal) -> None:
   337	        if principal.mode == "human":
   338	            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Not permitted for human principals.")
   339	
   340	    def _require_self_employee(principal: Principal, employee_id: str) -> None:
   341	        """human must act only as themselves; demo/system are unaffected here."""
   342	        if principal.mode == "human" and principal.employee_id != employee_id:
   343	            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Not authorized for this employee_id.")
   344	
   345	    def _require_self_agent(principal: Principal, agent_id: str, tenant_store: Store) -> None:
   346	        """human may only act through the agent bound to their own employee_id."""
   347	        if principal.mode != "human":
   348	            return
   349	        agent = tenant_store.get_agent(agent_id)
   350	        if agent is None or agent.employee_id != principal.employee_id:
   351	            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Not authorized for this agent_id.")
   352	
   353	    def _require_card_owner(principal: Principal, card_id: str, tenant_store: Store) -> None:
   354	        """human may only act on secretary cards they own; missing cards fall
   355	        through to the service layer's own 404 handling."""
   356	        if principal.mode != "human":
   357	            return
   358	        card = tenant_store.get_card(card_id)
   359	        if card is None:
   360	            return
   361	        if card.owner_employee_id != principal.employee_id:
   362	            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Not authorized for this card.")
   363	
   364	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   365	    # Web UI Routes (HTML Static Pages)
   366	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   367

   471	    def get_requester_status(

   473	        principal: Principal = Depends(get_principal),
   474	        ctx: TenantContext = Depends(get_context),
   475	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   476	        """Return requester-facing status projection (design.md §3, §6.4).
   477	
   478	        Strict Privacy Rules:
   479	        - When pending: Candidate employee_id and identity are NOT exposed.
   480	        - When resolved (matched or declined): Respondent ID/name and reason/attachment are exposed.
   481	        - NEVER exposes connect_ask vs connect_ask_private distinction or internal consent events.
   482	        """
   483	        _deny_system(principal)
   484	        _require_self_employee(principal, requester_id)
   485	        raw_statuses = ctx.service.get_requester_status(requester_id=requester_id)
   486	
   487	        formatted_statuses: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   488	        for idx, s in enumerate(raw_statuses, start=1):
   489	            if s.state == "pending":
   490	                formatted_statuses.append({
   491	                    "status_id": f"status_{idx}",
   492	                    "state": "pending",
   493	                    "display_state": "Waiting for response",
   494	                })
   495	            elif s.state == "matched":
   496	                formatted_statuses.append({
   497	                    "status_id": f"status_{idx}",
   498	                    "state": "matched",
   499	                    "display_state": "Connected (15-min Meeting Proposed)",
   500	                    "respondent_id": s.candidate_id,
   501	                    "respondent_name": s.candidate_name,
   502	                    "meeting_duration": s.meeting_duration or 15,
   503	                })
   504	            elif s.state == "declined":
   505	                formatted_statuses.append({
   506	                    "status_id": f"status_{idx}",
   507	                    "state": "declined",
   508	                    "display_state": "Unavailable this time",
   509	                    "respondent_id": s.candidate_id,
   510	                    "respondent_name": s.candidate_name,
   511	                    "decline_reason": s.decline_reason,
   512	                    "attachment": s.attachment,
   513	                })
   514	
   515	        return {
   516	            "requester_id": requester_id,
   517	            "statuses": formatted_statuses,
   518	        }

 def create_app_from_env() -> Any:
     """Production factory: wire FirestoreStore / Gemini adapters from environment.
 
     Env vars:
-    - USE_FIRESTORE=1        -> FirestoreStore (project from GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT)
-    - GEMINI_API_KEY set     -> GeminiEmbedder + GeminiConnectionInferencer
-    - DEMO_API_KEY           -> API key protection (create_app default)
+    - TENANTS_JSON           -> tenant ledger (default: 1 tenant, see tenancy.py)
+    - USE_FIRESTORE=1        -> FirestoreStore per tenant, database=<tenant.database> (§16.2)
+    - GEMINI_API_KEY set     -> GeminiEmbedder + GeminiConnectionInferencer (shared across tenants)
+    - DEMO_API_KEY           -> default tenant's API key when TENANTS_JSON is unset
 
     Run with: uvicorn 'knowledge_discovery.server:create_app_from_env' --factory
     """

+    project = os.environ.get("GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT")
 
-        store = FirestoreStore(project=os.environ.get("GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT"))

+        if use_firestore:

+            return FirestoreStore(project=project, database=tenant.database)

 def create_app(
     store: Store | None = None,
     service: KnowledgeDiscoveryService | None = None,
     api_key: str | None = None,
     llm_client: Any | None = None,
+    principal_resolver: PrincipalResolver | None = None,
+    registry: TenantRegistry | None = None,
+    store_factory: Callable[[TenantConfig], Store] | None = None,
+    matching_engine: MatchingEngine | None = None,
 ) -> Any:
-    """Create and configure the FastAPI application."""
+    """Create and configure the FastAPI application.
+
+    Tenancy (design §16.2): every tenant gets its own Store + KnowledgeDiscoveryService
+    + SecretaryService, built lazily and cached per-tenant by a ContextRouter; routes
+    below resolve `principal.tenant_id` and operate only on that tenant's context.
+
+    Test-friendly single-tenant compatibility: when `registry` is omitted, a
+    single-tenant registry (tenant_id="meridian") is built from `api_key`/
+    `DEMO_API_KEY`, and an explicitly passed `store=`/`service=` is used as
+    that one tenant's context (current single-tenant behavior, unchanged).
+    For a real multi-tenant ledger, pass `registry=` (e.g. TenantRegistry.from_env())
+    and `store_factory=` (how to build a fresh Store for a given tenant); `store=`/
+    `service=` are ignored in that case.
+    """
     if FastAPI is None:
         raise RuntimeError("fastapi is not installed. Please install requirements.txt.")
 
     expected_api_key = api_key or os.environ.get("DEMO_API_KEY", DEFAULT_DEMO_API_KEY)
 
-    # Initialize store and service if not provided
-    if store is None:
-        store = InMemoryStore()
-        # Seed the in-memory store with default personas and profiles
+    preset_store: Store | None = None
+    preset_service: KnowledgeDiscoveryService | None = None
+    if registry is None:
+        registry = TenantRegistry.single(
+            tenant_id=DEFAULT_TENANT_ID,
+            database=DEFAULT_DATABASE,
+            email_domains=list(DEFAULT_EMAIL_DOMAINS),
+            api_key=expected_api_key,
+        )
+        preset_store = store
+        preset_service = service
+
+    def _default_store_factory(_tenant: TenantConfig) -> Store:
+        new_store = InMemoryStore()
         from scripts.generate_seeds import populate_store
-        populate_store(store, dry_run=False)
 
-    if service is None:
-        matching_engine = MatchingEngine(
+        populate_store(new_store, dry_run=False)
+        return new_store
+
+    effective_store_factory = store_factory or _default_store_factory
+
+    # Gemini/embedding clients are stateless, so one MatchingEngine instance
+    # is shared across every tenant's context (§16.2); only the Store differs
+    # per tenant.
+    shared_matching_engine = matching_engine
+    if shared_matching_engine is None and preset_service is not None:
+        shared_matching_engine = preset_service.matching_engine
+    if shared_matching_engine is None:
+        shared_matching_engine = MatchingEngine(
             embedder=DeterministicEmbedder(),
             inferencer=FakeConnectionInferencer(),
             vector_floor=0.20,
             connection_threshold=0.50,
             max_dispatch_k=3,
             funnel_limit=20,
         )
-        service = KnowledgeDiscoveryService(store=store, matching_engine=matching_engine)
+
+    def _build_tenant_context(tenant: TenantConfig) -> TenantContext:
+        if preset_store is not None and tenant.tenant_id == DEFAULT_TENANT_ID:
+            tenant_store = preset_store
+        else:
+            tenant_store = effective_store_factory(tenant)
+
+        if preset_service is not None and tenant.tenant_id == DEFAULT_TENANT_ID:
+            tenant_service = preset_service
+        else:
+            tenant_service = KnowledgeDiscoveryService(store=tenant_store, matching_engine=shared_matching_engine)
+
+        tenant_secretary = SecretaryService(
+            store=tenant_store,
+            kd_service=tenant_service,
+            matching_engine=shared_matching_engine,
+            llm_client=llm_client,
+            # §16.3 data-source seam: SOURCE_CONNECTOR=seed|google_workspace (default seed).
+            # One connector instance per tenant context; SeedConnector is a no-op.
+            connector=build_connector_from_env(),
+        )
+        return TenantContext(
+            tenant=tenant,
+            store=tenant_store,
+            service=tenant_service,
+            secretary=tenant_secretary,
+            matching=shared_matching_engine,
+            static_counts={},
+        )
+
+    router = ContextRouter(registry, context_factory=_build_tenant_context)
 
     app = FastAPI(
         title="Knowledge Discovery API",
         description="Tacit knowledge discovery and synergy matching engine (Milestone 2)",
         version="0.2.0",
         # No unauthenticated surface beyond the UI shells (S-2): API schema
         # endpoints are disabled rather than left open on the public demo URL
         docs_url=None,
         redoc_url=None,
         openapi_url=None,
     )
 
     web_dir = Path(__file__).parent / "web"
 
-    # API Key verification dependency
-    def verify_api_key(
-        x_api_key: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-API-Key"),
-        api_key_query: str | None = Query(default=None, alias="api_key"),
-    ) -> str:
-        provided_key = x_api_key or api_key_query
-        if not provided_key or provided_key != expected_api_key:
-            raise HTTPException(
-                status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
-                detail="Invalid or missing API key. Provide header 'X-API-Key' or query parameter 'api_key'.",
-            )
-        return provided_key
+    # Principal resolution dependency (design §16.1/§16.2): AUTH_MODE=demo_key
+    # (default) reproduces the old X-API-Key/api_key check, now resolving the
+    # tenant from whichever tenant's key matched; AUTH_MODE=iap verifies the
+    # IAP-signed assertion instead. Every route below depends on this single
+    # function and then applies the §16.1 permission table.
+    resolver = principal_resolver or build_principal_resolver(registry, router)
+
+    def get_principal(request: Request) -> Principal:
+        return resolver.resolve(request)
+
+    def get_context(principal: Principal = Depends(get_principal)) -> TenantContext:
+        """Resolve the caller's own TenantContext (§16.2). This is the only
+        place a route obtains a store/service/secretary -- there is no
+        function that returns another tenant's context."""
+        try:
+            return router.for_tenant(principal.tenant_id)
+        except KeyError:
+            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Unknown tenant.")
+
+    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
+    # §16.1 permission-table helpers (default-deny: every route below states
+    # explicitly what each of demo/human/system may do; nothing is implicit)
+    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+    def _deny_system(principal: Principal) -> None:
+        if principal.mode == "system":
+            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Not permitted for system principals.")
+
+    def _deny_human(principal: Principal) -> None:
+        if principal.mode == "human":
+            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Not permitted for human principals.")
+
+    def _require_self_employee(principal: Principal, employee_id: str) -> None:
+        """human must act only as themselves; demo/system are unaffected here."""
+        if principal.mode == "human" and principal.employee_id != employee_id:
+            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Not authorized for this employee_id.")
+
+    def _require_self_agent(principal: Principal, agent_id: str, tenant_store: Store) -> None:
+        """human may only act through the agent bound to their own employee_id."""
+        if principal.mode != "human":
+            return
+        agent = tenant_store.get_agent(agent_id)
+        if agent is None or agent.employee_id != principal.employee_id:
+            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Not authorized for this agent_id.")
+
+    def _require_card_owner(principal: Principal, card_id: str, tenant_store: Store) -> None:
+        """human may only act on secretary cards they own; missing cards fall
+        through to the service layer's own 404 handling."""
+        if principal.mode != "human":
+            return
+        card = tenant_store.get_card(card_id)
+        if card is None:
+            return
+        if card.owner_employee_id != principal.employee_id:
+            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Not authorized for this card.")
 
     # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
     # Web UI Routes (HTML Static Pages)
     # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 
     @app.get("/", response_class=RedirectResponse, include_in_schema=False)
     def index() -> str:
         return "/requester"
 
     @app.get("/requester", response_class=HTMLResponse, include_in_schema=False)
     def requester_ui() -> HTMLResponse:
         html_file = web_dir / "requester.html"
         if html_file.exists():
             return HTMLResponse(content=html_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
         return HTMLResponse("<h1>Requester UI</h1><p>HTML file not found.</p>")
 
     @app.get("/candidate", response_class=HTMLResponse, include_in_schema=False)
     def candidate_ui() -> HTMLResponse:
         html_file = web_dir / "candidate.html"
         if html_file.exists():
             return HTMLResponse(content=html_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
         return HTMLResponse("<h1>Candidate UI</h1><p>HTML file not found.</p>")
 
     @app.get("/audit", response_class=HTMLResponse, include_in_schema=False)
     def audit_ui() -> HTMLResponse:
         html_file = web_dir / "audit.html"
         if html_file.exists():
             return HTMLResponse(content=html_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
         return HTMLResponse("<h1>Audit UI</h1><p>HTML file not found.</p>")
 
     # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
     # Static Document Delivery (C-24)

     @app.get("/attachments/{doc_id}", response_class=PlainTextResponse)
     def get_attachment(doc_id: str) -> PlainTextResponse:
         """Serve static document attachment by ID."""
         doc = SAMPLE_ATTACHMENTS.get(doc_id)
         if doc is None:
             raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Document attachment '{doc_id}' not found")
         return PlainTextResponse(content=doc, media_type="text/markdown")

   470	    @app.get("/api/requester/{requester_id}/status")
   471	    def get_requester_status(
   472	        requester_id: str,
   473	        principal: Principal = Depends(get_principal),
   474	        ctx: TenantContext = Depends(get_context),
   475	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   476	        """Return requester-facing status projection (design.md §3, §6.4).
   477	
   478	        Strict Privacy Rules:
   479	        - When pending: Candidate employee_id and identity are NOT exposed.
   480	        - When resolved (matched or declined): Respondent ID/name and reason/attachment are exposed.
   481	        - NEVER exposes connect_ask vs connect_ask_private distinction or internal consent events.
   482	        """
   483	        _deny_system(principal)
   484	        _require_self_employee(principal, requester_id)
   485	        raw_statuses = ctx.service.get_requester_status(requester_id=requester_id)
   486	
   487	        formatted_statuses: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   488	        for idx, s in enumerate(raw_statuses, start=1):
   489	            if s.state == "pending":
   490	                formatted_statuses.append({
   491	                    "status_id": f"status_{idx}",
   492	                    "state": "pending",
   493	                    "display_state": "Waiting for response",
   494	                })
   495	            elif s.state == "matched":
   496	                formatted_statuses.append({
   497	                    "status_id": f"status_{idx}",
   498	                    "state": "matched",
   499	                    "display_state": "Connected (15-min Meeting Proposed)",
   500	                    "respondent_id": s.candidate_id,
   501	                    "respondent_name": s.candidate_name,
   502	                    "meeting_duration": s.meeting_duration or 15,
   503	                })
   504	            elif s.state == "declined":
   505	                formatted_statuses.append({
   506	                    "status_id": f"status_{idx}",
   507	                    "state": "declined",
   508	                    "display_state": "Unavailable this time",
   509	                    "respondent_id": s.candidate_id,
   510	                    "respondent_name": s.candidate_name,
   511	                    "decline_reason": s.decline_reason,
   512	                    "attachment": s.attachment,
   513	                })
   514	
   515	        return {
   516	            "requester_id": requester_id,
   517	            "statuses": formatted_statuses,
   518	        }

   557	    @app.post("/api/candidate/{agent_id}/consent")
   558	    def submit_candidate_consent(
   559	        agent_id: str,
   560	        req: ConsentRequest,
   561	        principal: Principal = Depends(get_principal),
   562	        ctx: TenantContext = Depends(get_context),
   563	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   564	        """Submit candidate consent reply (granted or declined with optional attachment)."""
   565	        _deny_system(principal)
   566	        _require_self_agent(principal, agent_id, ctx.store)
   567	        att = None
   568	        if req.attachment is not None:
   569	            att = Attachment(type=req.attachment.type, content=req.attachment.content)
   570	
   571	        try:
   572	            consent_res = ctx.service.respond_consent(
   573	                candidate_entity_id=agent_id,
   574	                ask_audit_id=req.ask_audit_id,
   575	                decision=req.decision,
   576	                reason_text=req.reason_text,
   577	                attachment=att,
   578	            )
   579	            return {
   580	                "status": "ok",
   581	                "decision": req.decision,
   582	                "reply_audit_id": consent_res.reply_message.audit_id,
   583	                "outcome_intent": consent_res.outcome_message.intent,
   584	            }
   585	        except ValueError as exc:
   586	            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(exc))
   587	        except ConsentForbiddenError as exc:
   588	            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=str(exc))
   589	        except ConsentConflictError as exc:
   590	            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=str(exc))

   593	    # embeddings, ~10MB) from Firestore every 3 seconds (E-4). The counts are
   594	    # static for the demo, so compute them once on first request -- per
   595	    # tenant (ctx.static_counts), since tenants have independent profile/agent
   596	    # counts (§16.2).
   597	    @app.get("/api/audit/messages")
   598	    def get_audit_messages(
   599	        principal: Principal = Depends(get_principal), ctx: TenantContext = Depends(get_context)
   600	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   601	        """Retrieve audit dashboard records with fail-closed masked payloads and funnel stats."""
   602	        records = ctx.service.get_audit_dashboard_records()
   603	        if not ctx.static_counts:
   604	            ctx.static_counts["profiles"] = len(ctx.store.list_profiles())
   605	            ctx.static_counts["agents"] = len(ctx.store.list_agents(active_only=True))
   606	
   607	        dispatched_count = sum(1 for r in records if r["intent"] in ("connect_ask", "connect_ask_private"))
   608	        dropped_count = sum(1 for r in records if r["intent"] == "no_connection")
   609	
   610	        return {
   611	            "funnel_stats": {
   612	                "total_profiles": ctx.static_counts["profiles"],
   613	                "funnel_limit": 20,
   614	                "registered_agents_count": ctx.static_counts["agents"],
   615	                "dispatched_count": dispatched_count,
   616	                "dropped_count": dropped_count,
   617	            },
   618	            "records": records,
   619	        }

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba src/knowledge_discovery/service.py | sed -n '1,260p'" in /Users/toshixa/dev/Knowledge-discovery
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""High-level knowledge discovery coordinator service (Milestone 1).
     2	
     3	Follows design.md §1 - §7:
     4	- Orchestrates query submission, 2-track 2-stage matching, and asks dispatch.
     5	- Handles consent replies (granted -> match_proposal without reason_text; declined -> decline_with_reason).
     6	- Provides requester-facing status view (never distinguishing private from public asks).
     7	- Provides audit dashboard view with fail-closed payload masking.
     8	"""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    13	from typing import Any
    14	
    15	from knowledge_discovery.matching import (
    16	    DroppedCandidate,
    17	    FunnelCandidate,
    18	    MatchingEngine,
    19	)
    20	from knowledge_discovery.models import (
    21	    Attachment,
    22	    Message,
    23	    Profile,
    24	    RequesterCandidateStatus,
    25	)
    26	from knowledge_discovery.schemas import SchemaRegistry
    27	from knowledge_discovery.store import Store
    28	from knowledge_discovery.transmission import TransmissionLayer
    29	
    30	
    31	@dataclass
    32	class QuerySubmissionResult:
    33	    """Output of a submitted knowledge discovery query."""
    34	
    35	    query_message: Message
    36	    funnel_candidates: list[FunnelCandidate]
    37	    dispatched_asks: list[Message]
    38	    dropped_candidates: list[DroppedCandidate]
    39	
    40	
    41	class ConsentForbiddenError(Exception):
    42	    """Ask does not belong to this candidate (to_entity mismatch or non-connect_ask intent, W-1)."""
    43	
    44	
    45	class ConsentConflictError(Exception):
    46	    """Ask is not 'pending' (e.g. a duplicate consent POST after the first already resolved it, W-1)."""
    47	
    48	
    49	@dataclass
    50	class ConsentResult:
    51	    """Output of processing a candidate consent reply."""
    52	
    53	    reply_message: Message
    54	    outcome_message: Message  # match_proposal or decline_with_reason
    55	
    56	
    57	class KnowledgeDiscoveryService:
    58	    """Core service coordinating matching, transmission, consent, and audit."""
    59	
    60	    def __init__(
    61	        self,
    62	        store: Store,
    63	        transmission: TransmissionLayer | None = None,
    64	        matching_engine: MatchingEngine | None = None,
    65	    ) -> None:
    66	        self.store = store
    67	        self.transmission = transmission or TransmissionLayer(store)
    68	        self.matching_engine = matching_engine or MatchingEngine()
    69	        # No in-process requester/ask maps (V-2): Cloud Run scales to zero and
    70	        # restarts between requests, so every link lives in message payloads
    71	        # (requester_id / query_id / ask_audit_id) and is derived from the store.
    72	
    73	    def submit_query(self, requester_id: str, question_text: str) -> QuerySubmissionResult:
    74	        """Submit a question, run 2-track matching, and dispatch asks/no_connection records."""
    75	        # 1. Record query message
    76	        query_msg = self.transmission.send(
    77	            from_entity=requester_id,
    78	            to_entity="system",
    79	            intent="query",
    80	            payload_type="query",
    81	            payload={"question_text": question_text, "requester_id": requester_id},
    82	        )
    83	
    84	        # 2. Retrieve registered agents and all employee profiles
    85	        agents = self.store.list_agents(active_only=True)
    86	        profiles = self.store.list_profiles()
    87	
    88	        # 3. Run exploratory matching (2 tracks × 2 stages)
    89	        matching_res = self.matching_engine.run_matching(question_text, agents, profiles)
    90	
    91	        # 4. Record dropped candidates as no_connection in audit log
    92	        for dropped in matching_res.dropped_candidates:
    93	            self.transmission.send(
    94	                from_entity="system",
    95	                to_entity=dropped.agent.agent_id,
    96	                intent="no_connection",
    97	                payload_type="no_connection",
    98	                payload={
    99	                    "reason_text": dropped.reason_text,
   100	                    "cited_item_keys": list(dropped.cited_item_keys),
   101	                    "score": dropped.score,
   102	                },
   103	                candidate_profile=dropped.profile,
   104	            )
   105	
   106	        # 5. Dispatch asks to qualified candidates (up to max k=3)
   107	        dispatched_asks: list[Message] = []
   108	        for qual in matching_res.qualified_candidates:
   109	            ask_msg = self.transmission.send(
   110	                from_entity="system",
   111	                to_entity=qual.agent.agent_id,
   112	                intent="connect_ask",
   113	                payload_type="connect_ask",
   114	                payload={
   115	                    "question_summary": question_text,
   116	                    "reason_text": qual.reason_text,
   117	                    "cited_item_keys": list(qual.cited_item_keys),
   118	                    "score": qual.score,
   119	                    # Durable links (V-2): survive instance restarts
   120	                    "requester_id": requester_id,
   121	                    "query_id": query_msg.audit_id,
   122	                },
   123	                consent_state="pending",
   124	                candidate_profile=qual.profile,
   125	            )
   126	            dispatched_asks.append(ask_msg)
   127	
   128	        return QuerySubmissionResult(
   129	            query_message=query_msg,
   130	            funnel_candidates=matching_res.funnel_candidates,
   131	            dispatched_asks=dispatched_asks,
   132	            dropped_candidates=matching_res.dropped_candidates,
   133	        )
   134	
   135	    def respond_consent(
   136	        self,
   137	        candidate_entity_id: str,
   138	        ask_audit_id: str,
   139	        decision: str,  # 'granted' | 'declined'
   140	        reason_text: str = "",
   141	        attachment: Attachment | dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   142	    ) -> ConsentResult:
   143	        """Process a candidate's consent decision (granted or declined).
   144	
   145	        The ask->decision transition is verified and performed atomically
   146	        (W-1, design §16.1): the ask must exist, address this candidate,
   147	        carry a connect_ask* intent, and still be 'pending'. A second POST
   148	        against the same ask_audit_id (double-submit) raises ConsentConflictError.
   149	        """
   150	        original_ask, outcome = self.store.try_transition_ask_consent(
   151	            ask_audit_id, candidate_entity_id, decision
   152	        )
   153	        if outcome == "not_found":
   154	            raise ValueError(f"Ask message with ID '{ask_audit_id}' not found")
   155	        if outcome == "forbidden":
   156	            raise ConsentForbiddenError(
   157	                f"Ask '{ask_audit_id}' does not belong to candidate '{candidate_entity_id}'"
   158	            )
   159	        if outcome == "conflict":
   160	            state = original_ask.consent_state if original_ask else "?"
   161	            raise ConsentConflictError(
   162	                f"Ask '{ask_audit_id}' is no longer pending (current state: {state})"
   163	            )
   164	        assert original_ask is not None  # outcome == "ok"
   165	
   166	        # 1. Record consent_reply message
   167	        reply_msg = self.transmission.send(
   168	            from_entity=candidate_entity_id,
   169	            to_entity="system",
   170	            intent="consent_reply",
   171	            payload_type="consent_reply",
   172	            payload={"decision": decision, "ask_audit_id": ask_audit_id},
   173	            consent_state=decision,
   174	        )
   175	
   176	        # Requester is derived from the durable link in the ask payload (V-2)
   177	        requester_id = str(original_ask.payload.get("requester_id") or "requester")
   178	
   179	        # 2. Outcome handling based on decision
   180	        if decision == "granted":
   181	            # Match established -> match_proposal delivered to BOTH parties
   182	            # (design §6 / V-5: one message per recipient, no reason_text)
   183	            candidate_employee_id = candidate_entity_id
   184	            agent = self.store.get_agent(candidate_entity_id)
   185	            if agent is not None:
   186	                candidate_employee_id = agent.employee_id
   187	
   188	            proposal_payload = {
   189	                "meeting_duration": 15,
   190	                "proposed_by": "system",
   191	                "participants": [requester_id, candidate_employee_id],
   192	                "ask_audit_id": ask_audit_id,
   193	            }
   194	            outcome_msg = self.transmission.send(
   195	                from_entity="system",
   196	                to_entity=requester_id,
   197	                intent="match_proposal",
   198	                payload_type="match_proposal",
   199	                payload=dict(proposal_payload),
   200	                consent_state="granted",
   201	            )
   202	            self.transmission.send(
   203	                from_entity="system",
   204	                to_entity=candidate_employee_id,
   205	                intent="match_proposal",
   206	                payload_type="match_proposal",
   207	                payload=dict(proposal_payload),
   208	                consent_state="granted",
   209	            )
   210	        else:
   211	            # Declined -> return decline_with_reason to requester with reason and optional attachment
   212	            att_dict = None
   213	            if attachment is not None:
   214	                att_dict = attachment.to_dict() if isinstance(attachment, Attachment) else dict(attachment)
   215	
   216	            outcome_msg = self.transmission.send(
   217	                from_entity=candidate_entity_id,
   218	                to_entity=requester_id,
   219	                intent="decline_with_reason",
   220	                payload_type="decline_with_reason",
   221	                payload={
   222	                    "reason_text": reason_text,
   223	                    "attachment": att_dict,
   224	                    "ask_audit_id": ask_audit_id,
   225	                },
   226	                consent_state="declined",
   227	            )
   228	
   229	        return ConsentResult(reply_message=reply_msg, outcome_message=outcome_msg)
   230	
   231	    def get_requester_status(self, requester_id: str, query_audit_id: str | None = None) -> list[RequesterCandidateStatus]:
   232	        """Return candidate status list from the requester viewpoint (§6.4).
   233	
   234	        States:
   235	        - pending: '返答待ち'
   236	        - matched: 'つながりました（MTG提案あり）'
   237	        - declined: '今回は難しいそうです（理由+添付表示）'
   238	
   239	        Strict requirement: Does NOT distinguish connect_ask vs connect_ask_private.
   240	        """
   241	        # Store-derived, stateless projection (V-2). Scoped to ONE query so
   242	        # candidates from past questions do not mix into the view (V-4):
   243	        # the given query_audit_id, or the requester's latest query by default.
   244	        messages = self.store.list_messages()
   245	
   246	        if query_audit_id is None:
   247	            own_queries = [
   248	                m for m in messages
   249	                if m.intent == "query" and m.payload.get("requester_id") == requester_id
   250	            ]
   251	            if not own_queries:
   252	                return []
   253	            query_audit_id = max(own_queries, key=lambda m: m.timestamp).audit_id
   254	
   255	        asks = [
   256	            m for m in messages
   257	            if m.intent in ("connect_ask", "connect_ask_private")
   258	            and m.payload.get("query_id") == query_audit_id
   259	        ]
   260

     1	"""Storage abstraction and in-memory implementation for knowledge discovery.
     2	
     3	Follows design.md §3:
     4	- Store interface defining contracts for agents, profiles, and messages.
     5	- InMemoryStore providing a standalone, testable implementation without Firestore dependency.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
    11	from copy import deepcopy
    12	import threading
    13	from typing import Any
    14	
    15	from knowledge_discovery.models import (
    16	    Agent,
    17	    Card,

    27	class Store(ABC):
    28	    """Abstract storage interface for knowledge discovery entities."""
    29	
    30	    # Agent operations (agents collection / registry)
    31	    @abstractmethod
    32	    def save_agent(self, agent: Agent) -> None:
    33	        """Save or update an agent record in the registry."""
    34	        pass
    35	
    36	    @abstractmethod
    37	    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    38	        """Retrieve an agent by agent_id."""
    39	        pass
    40	
    41	    @abstractmethod
    42	    def get_agent_by_employee_id(self, employee_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    43	        """Retrieve an agent by employee_id."""
    44	        pass
    45	
    46	    @abstractmethod
    47	    def list_agents(self, active_only: bool = False) -> list[Agent]:
    48	        """List registered agents, optionally filtering for active only."""
    49	        pass
    50	
    51	    # Profile operations (profiles collection)
    52	    @abstractmethod
    53	    def save_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> None:
    54	        """Save or update an employee knowledge profile."""
    55	        pass
    56	
    57	    @abstractmethod
    58	    def get_profile(self, employee_id: str) -> Profile | None:
    59	        """Retrieve a profile by employee_id."""
    60	        pass
    61	
    62	    @abstractmethod
    63	    def list_profiles(self) -> list[Profile]:
    64	        """List all employee profiles."""
    65	        pass
    66	
    67	    # Message operations (messages collection / audit log)
    68	    @abstractmethod
    69	    def save_message(self, message: Message) -> None:
    70	        """Save or update a message record."""
    71	        pass
    72	
    73	    @abstractmethod
    74	    def get_message(self, audit_id: str) -> Message | None:
    75	        """Retrieve a message by audit_id."""
    76	        pass
    77	
    78	    @abstractmethod
    79	    def list_messages(self, limit: int | None = None) -> list[Message]:
    80	        """List all messages in chronological order."""
    81	        pass
    82	
    83	    @abstractmethod
    84	    def get_messages_for_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> list[Message]:
    85	        """Retrieve messages involving the given entity (as sender, recipient, or participant)."""
    86	        pass
    87	
    88	    # Task operations (tasks collection §14.2)
    89	    @abstractmethod
    90	    def save_task(self, task: Task) -> None:
    91	        """Save or update a task record."""
    92	        pass
    93	
    94	    @abstractmethod
    95	    def get_task(self, task_id: str) -> Task | None:
    96	        """Retrieve a task by task_id."""
    97	        pass

   100	    def list_tasks(
   101	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, source: str | None = None
   102	    ) -> list[Task]:
   103	        """List tasks, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id and/or source."""
   104	        pass
   105

   109	        """Save or update a schedule reminder."""
   110	        pass

   114	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, source: str | None = None
   115	    ) -> list[Schedule]:
   116	        """List schedules, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id and/or source."""
   117	        pass
   118	
   119	    @abstractmethod

   121	        """Delete a schedule reminder by item_id (§16.3 Calendar reconciliation).
   122	
   123	        No-op if the item_id doesn't exist.

   125	        pass

   131	        pass
   132
