   136	        pass
   137

   143	        pass

   157	        pass
   158

   161	        """Retrieve a card by card_id."""
   162	        pass
   163

   165	    def list_cards(
   166	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, status: str | None = None
   167	    ) -> list[Card]:
   168	        """List cards, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id and/or status."""
   169	        pass
   170	
   171	    @abstractmethod
   172	    def find_open_card_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> Card | None:
   173	        """Find an open stagnation card for the given task and owner."""
   174	        pass
   175	
   176	    @abstractmethod
   177	    def find_cards_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> list[Card]:
   178	        """Find all stagnation cards for the given task and owner."""
   179	        pass
   180	
   181	    @abstractmethod
   182	    def try_confirm_card(self, card_id: str) -> tuple[Card | None, bool]:
   183	        """Atomically transition a card's status from 'open' to 'confirmed' (CAS, §14.4).
   184	
   185	        Returns (card, won):
   186	        - (None, False) if no card with this card_id exists.
   187	        - (card, True) if THIS call performed the open->confirmed transition; the
   188	          returned card reflects the post-transition ('confirmed') state.
   189	        - (card, False) if the card exists but was not in 'open' status (already
   190	          'confirmed' by a concurrent caller, or 'dismissed'/'resolved'/'applied');
   191	          the returned card reflects its current stored state.
   192	
   193	        Implementations must guarantee at most one caller ever observes won=True
   194	        for a given card_id (Firestore: @firestore.transactional; in-memory: a
   195	        lock guarding the read-check-write).
   196	        """
   197	        pass
   198	
   199	    # Identity operations (identities collection, design §16.1 Part A)
   200	    @abstractmethod
   201	    def get_identity(self, email: str) -> str | None:
   202	        """Resolve a verified email to its employee_id, or None if unregistered."""
   203	        pass
   204	
   205	    @abstractmethod
   206	    def save_identity(self, email: str, employee_id: str) -> None:
   207	        """Register (or update) the employee_id bound to an email address."""
   208	        pass
   209	
   210	    @abstractmethod
   211	    def try_transition_ask_consent(
   212	        self, ask_audit_id: str, agent_id: str, new_consent_state: str
   213	    ) -> tuple[Message | None, str]:
   214	        """Atomically verify and transition an ask message's consent_state (W-1, §16.1).
   215	
   216	        Verifies: message exists, message.to_entity == agent_id, message.intent
   217	        is 'connect_ask' or 'connect_ask_private', and message.consent_state == 'pending'
   218	        -- then transitions consent_state to new_consent_state, all under one lock/transaction.
   219	
   220	        Returns (message, outcome), outcome one of:
   221	        - 'ok': transition performed; message reflects the new consent_state.
   222	        - 'not_found': no message with this audit_id.
   223	        - 'forbidden': message exists but to_entity != agent_id, or intent is
   224	          not a connect_ask variant.
   225	        - 'conflict': message exists and belongs to this agent, but consent_state
   226	          is not 'pending' (e.g. a duplicate POST after the first has resolved it).
   227	        """
   228	        pass
   229	
   230	    @abstractmethod
   231	    def clear(self) -> None:
   232	        """Clear all stored data (useful for test isolation)."""
   233	        pass
   234	
   235	
   236	class InMemoryStore(Store):
   237	    """In-memory implementation of Store using Python data structures."""
   238	
   239	    def __init__(self) -> None:
   240	        self._agents: dict[str, Agent] = {}
   241	        self._profiles: dict[str, Profile] = {}
   242	        self._messages: list[Message] = []
   243	        self._messages_by_id: dict[str, Message] = {}
   244	        self._tasks: dict[str, Task] = {}
   245	        self._schedules: dict[str, Schedule] = {}
   246	        self._mail_seeds: dict[str, MailSeed] = {}
   247	        self._cards: dict[str, Card] = {}
   248	        self._card_lock = threading.Lock()
   249	        self._identities: dict[str, str] = {}
   250	        self._consent_lock = threading.Lock()
   251	
   252	    def save_agent(self, agent: Agent) -> None:
   253	        """Save or update an agent record in the registry."""
   254	        stored = deepcopy(agent)
   255	        self._agents[stored.agent_id] = stored
   256	
   257	    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> Agent | None:
   258	        """Retrieve an agent by agent_id."""
   259	        agent = self._agents.get(agent_id)
   260	        return deepcopy(agent) if agent is not None else None
   261	
   262	    def get_agent_by_employee_id(self, employee_id: str) -> Agent | None:
   263	        """Retrieve an agent by employee_id."""
   264	        for agent in self._agents.values():
   265	            if agent.employee_id == employee_id:
   266	                return deepcopy(agent)
   267	        return None
   268	
   269	    def list_agents(self, active_only: bool = False) -> list[Agent]:
   270	        """List registered agents, optionally filtering for active only."""
   271	        agents = list(self._agents.values())
   272	        if active_only:
   273	            agents = [a for a in agents if a.active]
   274	        return [deepcopy(a) for a in agents]
   275	
   276	    def save_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> None:
   277	        """Save or update an employee knowledge profile."""
   278	        stored = deepcopy(profile)
   279	        self._profiles[stored.employee_id] = stored
   280	
   281	    def get_profile(self, employee_id: str) -> Profile | None:
   282	        """Retrieve a profile by employee_id."""
   283	        profile = self._profiles.get(employee_id)
   284	        return deepcopy(profile) if profile is not None else None
   285	
   286	    def list_profiles(self) -> list[Profile]:
   287	        """List all employee profiles."""
   288	        return [deepcopy(p) for p in self._profiles.values()]
   289	
   290	    def save_message(self, message: Message) -> None:
   291	        """Save or update a message record."""
   292	        stored = deepcopy(message)
   293	        if stored.audit_id in self._messages_by_id:
   294	            for idx, existing in enumerate(self._messages):
   295	                if existing.audit_id == stored.audit_id:
   296	                    self._messages[idx] = stored
   297	                    break
   298	        else:
   299	            self._messages.append(stored)
   300	        self._messages_by_id[stored.audit_id] = stored
   301	
   302	    def get_message(self, audit_id: str) -> Message | None:
   303	        """Retrieve a message by audit_id."""
   304	        msg = self._messages_by_id.get(audit_id)
   305	        return deepcopy(msg) if msg is not None else None
   306	
   307	    def list_messages(self, limit: int | None = None) -> list[Message]:
   308	        """List all messages in chronological order."""
   309	        messages = [deepcopy(m) for m in self._messages]
   310	        if limit is not None and limit > 0:
   311	            return messages[-limit:]
   312	        return messages
   313	
   314	    def get_messages_for_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> list[Message]:
   315	        """Retrieve messages involving the given entity (as sender, recipient, or participant)."""
   316	        results: list[Message] = []
   317	        for msg in self._messages:
   318	            participants = msg.payload.get("participants", []) if isinstance(msg.payload, dict) else []
   319	            if (
   320	                msg.from_entity == entity_id
   321	                or msg.to_entity == entity_id
   322	                or entity_id in participants
   323	            ):
   324	                results.append(deepcopy(msg))
   325	        return results
   326	
   327	    # Task operations
   328	    def save_task(self, task: Task) -> None:

   427	    def try_confirm_card(self, card_id: str) -> tuple[Card | None, bool]:
   428	        """Atomically transition a card's status from 'open' to 'confirmed' (CAS)."""
   429	        with self._card_lock:
   430	            card = self._cards.get(card_id)
   431	            if card is None:
   432	                return None, False
   433	            if card.status != "open":
   434	                return deepcopy(card), False
   435	            card.status = "confirmed"
   436	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   437	            self._cards[card.card_id] = deepcopy(card)
   438	            return deepcopy(card), True

   447	    def try_transition_ask_consent(
   448	        self, ask_audit_id: str, agent_id: str, new_consent_state: str
   449	    ) -> tuple[Message | None, str]:
   450	        """Atomically verify and transition an ask message's consent_state."""
   451	        with self._consent_lock:
   452	            msg = self._messages_by_id.get(ask_audit_id)
   453	            if msg is None:
   454	                return None, "not_found"
   455	            if msg.to_entity != agent_id or msg.intent not in ("connect_ask", "connect_ask_private"):
   456	                return deepcopy(msg), "forbidden"
   457	            if msg.consent_state != "pending":
   458	                return deepcopy(msg), "conflict"
   459	            msg.consent_state = new_consent_state
   460	            return deepcopy(msg), "ok"

     1	"""Firestore implementation of the Store interface for knowledge discovery.
     2	
     3	Follows design.md §3:
     4	- agents collection: agents/{agent_id}
     5	- profiles collection: profiles/{employee_id}
     6	- messages collection: messages/{audit_id}
     7	- In-memory vector similarity computation over fetched profiles (avoiding composite index validation uncertainties).
     8	"""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	from typing import Any
    13	
    14	from knowledge_discovery.models import (
    15	    Agent,
    16	    Card,
    17	    MailSeed,
    18	    Message,
    19	    Profile,
    20	    Schedule,
    21	    Task,
    22	    utc_now_iso,
    23	)
    24	from knowledge_discovery.store import Store
    25

    27	    from google.cloud import firestore
    28	except ImportError:
    29	    firestore = None  # type: ignore[assignment]
    30	
    31	
    32	class FirestoreStore(Store):
    33	    """Google Cloud Firestore implementation of Store interface."""
    34	
    35	    def __init__(
    36	        self,
    37	        client: Any | None = None,
    38	        project: str | None = None,
    39	        database: str | None = None,
    40	    ) -> None:
    41	        """Initialize FirestoreStore with either an injected client or new Firestore Client."""
    42	        if client is not None:
    43	            self.db = client
    44	        elif firestore is not None:
    45	            self.db = firestore.Client(project=project, database=database)
    46	        else:
    47	            raise RuntimeError(
    48	                "google-cloud-firestore is not installed and no custom client was provided."
    49	            )
    50	
    51	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52	    # Agent operations (agents collection / registry)
    53	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54	
    55	    def save_agent(self, agent: Agent) -> None:
    56	        """Save or update an agent record in the agents collection."""
    57	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("agents").document(agent.agent_id)
    58	        doc_ref.set(agent.to_dict())
    59	
    60	    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    61	        """Retrieve an agent by agent_id."""
    62	        doc = self.db.collection("agents").document(agent_id).get()
    63	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
    64	            return None
    65	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
    66	        if not data:
    67	            return None
    68	        return Agent.from_dict(data)
    69	
    70	    def get_agent_by_employee_id(self, employee_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    71	        """Retrieve an agent by employee_id."""
    72	        # Query agents collection for matching employee_id
    73	        col = self.db.collection("agents")
    74	        docs = list(col.where("employee_id", "==", employee_id).limit(1).stream())
    75	        if not docs:
    76	            return None
    77	        doc = docs[0]
    78	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
    79	        if not data:
    80	            return None
    81	        return Agent.from_dict(data)
    82	
    83	    def list_agents(self, active_only: bool = False) -> list[Agent]:
    84	        """List registered agents, optionally filtering for active only."""
    85	        col = self.db.collection("agents")
    86	        if active_only:
    87	            docs = col.where("active", "==", True).stream()
    88	        else:
    89	            docs = col.stream()
    90	
    91	        agents: list[Agent] = []
    92	        for doc in docs:
    93	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
    94	            if data:
    95	                agents.append(Agent.from_dict(data))
    96	        return agents

   137	    def get_message(self, audit_id: str) -> Message | None:
   138	        """Retrieve a message by audit_id."""
   139	        doc = self.db.collection("messages").document(audit_id).get()
   140	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:

   146	
   147	    def list_messages(self, limit: int | None = None) -> list[Message]:
   148	        """List all messages in chronological order."""
   149	        col = self.db.collection("messages")
   150	        # In Firestore, retrieve and sort in-memory or by timestamp
   151	        docs = col.stream()
   152	        messages: list[Message] = []
   153	        for doc in docs:
   154	            data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   155	            if data:
   156	                messages.append(Message.from_dict(data))
   157	
   158	        # Sort chronologically by timestamp
   159	        messages.sort(key=lambda m: m.timestamp)
   160	
   161	        if limit is not None and limit > 0:
   162	            return messages[-limit:]
   163	        return messages
   164	
   165	    def get_messages_for_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> list[Message]:
   166	        """Retrieve messages involving the given entity (sender, recipient, or participant)."""
   167	        all_msgs = self.list_messages()
   168	        results: list[Message] = []
   169	        for msg in all_msgs:
   170	            participants = (
   171	                msg.payload.get("participants", []) if isinstance(msg.payload, dict) else []
   172	            )
   173	            if (
   174	                msg.from_entity == entity_id
   175	                or msg.to_entity == entity_id
   176	                or entity_id in participants
   177	            ):
   178	                results.append(msg)
   179	        return results

   347	        docs = (

   349	            .where("type", "==", "stagnation")

   351	            .stream()
   352	        )
   353	        cards: list[Card] = []
   354	        for doc in docs:

   356	            if data:
   357	                cards.append(Card.from_dict(data))
   358	        return cards
   359	
   360	    def try_confirm_card(self, card_id: str) -> tuple[Card | None, bool]:
   361	        """Atomically transition a card's status from 'open' to 'confirmed' (CAS, §14.4)."""
   362	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("cards").document(card_id)
   363	        transaction = self.db.transaction()
   364	
   365	        @firestore.transactional
   366	        def _txn(txn: Any) -> tuple[Card | None, bool]:
   367	            snapshot = doc_ref.get(transaction=txn)
   368	            if hasattr(snapshot, "exists") and not snapshot.exists:
   369	                return None, False
   370	            data = snapshot.to_dict() if hasattr(snapshot, "to_dict") else None
   371	            if not data:
   372	                return None, False
   373	            card = Card.from_dict(data)
   374	            if card.status != "open":
   375	                return card, False
   376	            card.status = "confirmed"
   377	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   378	            txn.set(doc_ref, card.to_dict())
   379	            return card, True
   380	
   381	        return _txn(transaction)
   382	
   383	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   384	    # Identity operations (identities collection, design §16.1 Part A)
   385	    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   386	
   387	    def get_identity(self, email: str) -> str | None:
   388	        """Resolve a verified email to its employee_id, or None if unregistered."""
   389	        doc = self.db.collection("identities").document(email.strip().lower()).get()
   390	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
   391	            return None
   392	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   393	        if not data:
   394	            return None
   395	        return data.get("employee_id")
   396	
   397	    def save_identity(self, email: str, employee_id: str) -> None:
   398	        """Register (or update) the employee_id bound to an email address."""
   399	        normalized_email = email.strip().lower()
   400	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("identities").document(normalized_email)
   401	        doc_ref.set({"email": normalized_email, "employee_id": employee_id})
   402	
   403	    def try_transition_ask_consent(
   404	        self, ask_audit_id: str, agent_id: str, new_consent_state: str
   405	    ) -> tuple[Message | None, str]:
   406	        """Atomically verify and transition an ask message's consent_state (W-1, §16.1)."""
   407	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("messages").document(ask_audit_id)
   408	        transaction = self.db.transaction()
   409	
   410	        @firestore.transactional
   411	        def _txn(txn: Any) -> tuple[Message | None, str]:
   412	            snapshot = doc_ref.get(transaction=txn)
   413	            if hasattr(snapshot, "exists") and not snapshot.exists:
   414	                return None, "not_found"
   415	            data = snapshot.to_dict() if hasattr(snapshot, "to_dict") else None
   416	            if not data:
   417	                return None, "not_found"
   418	            msg = Message.from_dict(data)
   419	            if msg.to_entity != agent_id or msg.intent not in ("connect_ask", "connect_ask_private"):
   420	                return msg, "forbidden"
   421	            if msg.consent_state != "pending":
   422	                return msg, "conflict"
   423	            msg.consent_state = new_consent_state
   424	            txn.set(doc_ref, msg.to_dict())
   425	            return msg, "ok"
   426	
   427	        return _txn(transaction)
   428	
   429	    def clear(self) -> None:
   430	        """Clear all stored data across all collections."""
   431	        for col_name in (
   432	            "agents",
   433	            "profiles",
   434	            "messages",
   435	            "tasks",
   436	            "schedules",
   437	            "mail_seeds",
   438	            "cards",
   439	            "identities",
   440	        ):
   441	            col = self.db.collection(col_name)
   442	            docs = col.stream()
   443	            for doc in docs:
   444	                doc.reference.delete()

     def respond_consent(
         self,
         candidate_entity_id: str,
         ask_audit_id: str,
         decision: str,  # 'granted' | 'declined'
         reason_text: str = "",
         attachment: Attachment | dict[str, Any] | None = None,
     ) -> ConsentResult:
-        """Process a candidate's consent decision (granted or declined)."""
-        original_ask = self.store.get_message(ask_audit_id)
-        if original_ask is None:
-            raise ValueError(f"Ask message with ID '{ask_audit_id}' not found")
+        """Process a candidate's consent decision (granted or declined).
 
-        # Update original ask message consent state
-        original_ask.consent_state = decision
-        self.store.save_message(original_ask)
+        The ask->decision transition is verified and performed atomically
+        (W-1, design §16.1): the ask must exist, address this candidate,
+        carry a connect_ask* intent, and still be 'pending'. A second POST
+        against the same ask_audit_id (double-submit) raises ConsentConflictError.
+        """
+        original_ask, outcome = self.store.try_transition_ask_consent(
+            ask_audit_id, candidate_entity_id, decision
+        )
+        if outcome == "not_found":
+            raise ValueError(f"Ask message with ID '{ask_audit_id}' not found")
+        if outcome == "forbidden":
+            raise ConsentForbiddenError(
+                f"Ask '{ask_audit_id}' does not belong to candidate '{candidate_entity_id}'"
+            )
+        if outcome == "conflict":
+            state = original_ask.consent_state if original_ask else "?"
+            raise ConsentConflictError(
+                f"Ask '{ask_audit_id}' is no longer pending (current state: {state})"
+            )
+        assert original_ask is not None  # outcome == "ok"
 
         # 1. Record consent_reply message
         reply_msg = self.transmission.send(
             from_entity=candidate_entity_id,
             to_entity="system",
             intent="consent_reply",
             payload_type="consent_reply",
             payload={"decision": decision, "ask_audit_id": ask_audit_id},
             consent_state=decision,
         )
 
         # Requester is derived from the durable link in the ask payload (V-2)
         requester_id = str(original_ask.payload.get("requester_id") or "requester")
 
         # 2. Outcome handling based on decision
         if decision == "granted":
             # Match established -> match_proposal delivered to BOTH parties
             # (design §6 / V-5: one message per recipient, no reason_text)
             candidate_employee_id = candidate_entity_id
             agent = self.store.get_agent(candidate_entity_id)

     @abstractmethod
     def try_confirm_card(self, card_id: str) -> tuple[Card | None, bool]:
         """Atomically transition a card's status from 'open' to 'confirmed' (CAS, §14.4).
 
         Returns (card, won):
         - (None, False) if no card with this card_id exists.
         - (card, True) if THIS call performed the open->confirmed transition; the
           returned card reflects the post-transition ('confirmed') state.
         - (card, False) if the card exists but was not in 'open' status (already
           'confirmed' by a concurrent caller, or 'dismissed'/'resolved'/'applied');
           the returned card reflects its current stored state.
 
         Implementations must guarantee at most one caller ever observes won=True
         for a given card_id (Firestore: @firestore.transactional; in-memory: a
         lock guarding the read-check-write).
         """
         pass

     def save_agent(self, agent: Agent) -> None:
         """Save or update an agent record in the registry."""

     def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> Agent | None:
         """Retrieve an agent by agent_id."""
         agent = self._agents.get(agent_id)
         return deepcopy(agent) if agent is not None else None
 
     def get_agent_by_employee_id(self, employee_id: str) -> Agent | None:
         """Retrieve an agent by employee_id."""
         for agent in self._agents.values():
             if agent.employee_id == employee_id:
                 return deepcopy(agent)
         return None

     def get_messages_for_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> list[Message]:
         """Retrieve messages involving the given entity (as sender, recipient, or participant)."""
         results: list[Message] = []
         for msg in self._messages:
             participants = msg.payload.get("participants", []) if isinstance(msg.payload, dict) else []
             if (
                 msg.from_entity == entity_id
                 or msg.to_entity == entity_id
                 or entity_id in participants
             ):
                 results.append(deepcopy(msg))
         return results

     def try_confirm_card(self, card_id: str) -> tuple[Card | None, bool]:
         """Atomically transition a card's status from 'open' to 'confirmed' (CAS)."""
         with self._card_lock:
             card = self._cards.get(card_id)
             if card is None:
                 return None, False
             if card.status != "open":
                 return deepcopy(card), False
             card.status = "confirmed"
             card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
             self._cards[card.card_id] = deepcopy(card)
             return deepcopy(card), True

   387	    def get_identity(self, email: str) -> str | None:
   388	        """Resolve a verified email to its employee_id, or None if unregistered."""
   389	        doc = self.db.collection("identities").document(email.strip().lower()).get()
   390	        if hasattr(doc, "exists") and not doc.exists:
   391	            return None
   392	        data = doc.to_dict() if hasattr(doc, "to_dict") else None
   393	        if not data:
   394	            return None
   395	        return data.get("employee_id")

   399	        normalized_email = email.strip().lower()
   400	        doc_ref = self.db.collection("identities").document(normalized_email)
   401	        doc_ref.set({"email": normalized_email, "employee_id": employee_id})

   404	        self, ask_audit_id: str, agent_id: str, new_consent_state: str
   405	    ) -> tuple[Message | None, str]:
   406	        """Atomically verify and transition an ask message's consent_state (W-1, §16.1)."""

   409

   411	        def _txn(txn: Any) -> tuple[Message | None, str]:
   412	            snapshot = doc_ref.get(transaction=txn)
   413	            if hasattr(snapshot, "exists") and not snapshot.exists:
   414	                return None, "not_found"
   415	            data = snapshot.to_dict() if hasattr(snapshot, "to_dict") else None

   422	                return msg, "conflict"
   423	            msg.consent_state = new_consent_state
   424	            txn.set(doc_ref, msg.to_dict())
   425	            return msg, "ok"

   304	    """Orchestrates secretary background sweep, morning digest, card CAS, and diff reviews."""
   305	
   306	    def __init__(
   307	        self,
   308	        store: Store,

   323	        assert self.t1 < self.t2, f"t1 ({self.t1}) must be < t2 ({self.t2})"
   324	
   325	    def _sync_owners(
   326	        self, registered_agents: list[Any], profiles_list: list[Profile], ref_today: date
   327	    ) -> dict[str, int]:
   328	        """Sync-then-detect's "sync" half (§16.3): pull each tenant owner's
   329	        external data through `self.connector` and reconcile it via
   330	        `apply_fetch_result` before stagnation detection runs. `SeedConnector`
   331	        (the default) is a no-op, so this is a no-op in demo mode.
   332	
   333	        Single-owner mode (`GWS_SELF_EMPLOYEE_ID`): only that owner is
   334	        synced; every other owner is counted as skipped rather than synced.
   335	        A connector/reconciliation failure for one owner is caught and
   336	        counted so it never halts the sweep (§16.3 failure handling) — and
   337	        never logs task/mail titles or bodies.
   338	        """
   339	        today_str = ref_today.isoformat()
   340	        self_only = os.environ.get("GWS_SELF_EMPLOYEE_ID", "").strip()
   341	        target_owners = {a.employee_id for a in registered_agents} | {
   342	            p.employee_id for p in profiles_list
   343	        }
   344	
   345	        stats = {
   346	            "sync_tasks": 0,
   347	            "sync_schedules": 0,
   348	            "sync_mails": 0,
   349	            "sync_skipped": 0,
   350	            "sync_errors": 0,
   351	        }
   352	        for owner_id in sorted(target_owners):
   353	            if self_only and owner_id != self_only:
   354	                stats["sync_skipped"] += 1
   355	                continue
   356	            try:
   357	                fetch_result = self.connector.fetch(owner_id, today_str)
   358	                summary = apply_fetch_result(self.store, owner_id, fetch_result, today_str)
   359	            except Exception:
   360	                stats["sync_errors"] += 1
   361	                continue
   362	            stats["sync_tasks"] += summary.tasks
   363	            stats["sync_schedules"] += summary.schedules
   364	            stats["sync_mails"] += summary.mails
   365	            stats["sync_skipped"] += summary.skipped
   366	            stats["sync_errors"] += len(summary.errors)
   367	        return stats
   368	
   369	    def _apply_mail_retention(self, ref_today: date) -> None:
   370	        """Enforce mail_seed retention (§16.3 Gmail part D).
   371	
   372	        Runs every sweep regardless of connector: a mail whose body was
   373	        already cleared has nothing left to clear, and SeedConnector never
   374	        produces mail_seeds, so this is a no-op in demo mode.
   375	        - Any mail already `processed=True` with a non-empty body has its
   376	          body cleared (the diff proposal, if any, was already extracted
   377	          and written to a card; the raw body has no further use).
   378	        - Any mail (processed or not) older than MAIL_RETENTION_DAYS is
   379	          deleted outright.
   380	        """
   381	        for mail in self.store.list_mail_seeds():
   382	            received = _parse_date_or_timestamp(mail.received_at)
   383	            if received is not None and (ref_today - received).days >= MAIL_RETENTION_DAYS:
   384	                self.store.delete_mail_seed(mail.mail_id)
   385	                continue
   386	            if mail.processed and mail.body:
   387	                mail.body = ""
   388	                self.store.save_mail_seed(mail)
   389	
   390	    def run_sweep(self, demo_today: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
   391	        """Execute full secretary sweep across all tasks and mail seeds (§14.1, §14.2, §14.5).
   392	
   393	        Sync-then-detect (§16.3): the tenant's owners (agents registered +
   394	        profiles owners) are synced through `self.connector` first; stagnation
   395	        detection below then runs against whatever is in `Store` afterward
   396	        (seed data, previously-synced gws data, or freshly-synced gws data).
   397	
   398	        State Machine Rules (§14.2):
   399	        1. Confirmed or Dismissed tasks: Do NOT recreate cards.
   400	        2. Task status 'done' or score < T1: Transition open card to 'resolved'.
   401	        3. T1 <= score < T2: Create or update 'notice' card.
   402	        4. score >= T2: Run preview search (public only, embedding_public). If candidates found,
   403	           create or promote card to 'request_draft'. If 0 candidates, keep/create 'notice'.
   404	        5. Idempotent: Never creates duplicate open cards for the same (owner, task_id).
   405	        """
   406	        ref_today = get_today(demo_today)
   407	        registered_agents = self.store.list_agents(active_only=True)
   408	        profiles_list = self.store.list_profiles()
   409	        profiles_map = {p.employee_id: p for p in profiles_list}
   410	
   411	        sync_stats = self._sync_owners(registered_agents, profiles_list, ref_today)
   412	
   413	        all_tasks = self.store.list_tasks()
   414	
   415	        # Group tasks by owner_employee_id
   416	        tasks_by_owner: dict[str, list[Task]] = {}
   417	        for t in all_tasks:
   418	            tasks_by_owner.setdefault(t.owner_employee_id, []).append(t)
   419	
   420	        tasks_evaluated = 0
   421	        cards_created = 0
   422	        cards_promoted = 0
   423	        cards_updated = 0
   424	        cards_resolved = 0
   425	        diff_cards_created = 0
   426	
   427	        for owner_id, owner_tasks in tasks_by_owner.items():
   428	            # Resolve sender entity for audit recording
   429	            owner_agent = self.store.get_agent_by_employee_id(owner_id)
   430	            sender_entity = owner_agent.agent_id if owner_agent else owner_id
   431	
   432	            for task in owner_tasks:
   433	                tasks_evaluated += 1
   434	
   435	                # Rule 1: Skip if already confirmed or dismissed
   436	                existing_cards = self.store.find_cards_for_task(owner_id, task.task_id)
   437	                has_terminal = any(c.status in ("confirmed", "dismissed") for c in existing_cards)
   438	                if has_terminal:
   439	                    continue
   440	
   441	                open_card = self.store.find_open_card_for_task(owner_id, task.task_id)
   442	
   443	                # Rule 2: If task is done -> resolve open card
   444	                if task.status == "done":
   445	                    if open_card is not None:
   446	                        open_card.status = "resolved"
   447	                        open_card.resolved_reason = "task_done"
   448	                        open_card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   449	                        self.store.save_card(open_card)
   450	                        cards_resolved += 1
   451	                    continue
   452	
   453	                # Calculate score
   454	                score, evidence_line, _ = calculate_stagnation_score(
   455	                    task, owner_tasks, today=ref_today
   456	                )
   457	
   458	                # Rule 2b: If score < T1 -> resolve open card
   459	                if score < self.t1:
   460	                    if open_card is not None:
   461	                        open_card.status = "resolved"
   462	                        open_card.resolved_reason = "score_below_t1"
   463	                        open_card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   464	                        self.store.save_card(open_card)
   465	                        cards_resolved += 1
   466	                    continue
   467	
   468	                # Rule 3: T1 <= score < T2 (Notice Tier)
   469	                if self.t1 <= score < self.t2:
   470	                    if open_card is None:

   696	    def get_morning_digest(
   697	        self,
   698	        employee_id: str,
   699	        demo_today: str | None = None,
   700	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   701	        """Assemble dynamic morning digest for employee (§14.2, §14.8).
   702	
   703	        Schedule rule order: Overdue -> Today -> Tomorrow -> Upcoming.
   704	        Followed by open stagnation and profile_diff cards.
   705	        """
   706	        ref_today = get_today(demo_today)
   707	        all_schedules = self.store.list_schedules(owner_employee_id=employee_id)
   708	
   709	        # Categorize and order schedule reminders
   710	        reminders: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   711	        for s in all_schedules:
   712	            due_d = _parse_date_or_timestamp(s.due_date)
   713	            if due_d is None:
   714	                category = "upcoming"
   715	                priority = 4
   716	            elif due_d < ref_today:
   717	                category = "overdue"
   718	                priority = 1
   719	            elif due_d == ref_today:
   720	                category = "today"
   721	                priority = 2
   722	            elif (due_d - ref_today).days == 1:
   723	                category = "tomorrow"
   724	                priority = 3
   725	            else:
   726	                category = "upcoming"
   727	                priority = 4
   728	
   729	            reminders.append({
   730	                "item_id": s.item_id,
   731	                "kind": s.kind,
   732	                "title": s.title,
   733	                "due_date": s.due_date,
   734	                "due_category": category,
   735	                "_priority": priority,
   736	            })
   737	
   738	        # Sort: priority ascending, then due_date ascending
   739	        reminders.sort(key=lambda r: (r["_priority"], r["due_date"]))
   740	        for r in reminders:
   741	            r.pop("_priority", None)
   742	
   743	        # Retrieve open cards
   744	        open_cards = self.store.list_cards(owner_employee_id=employee_id, status="open")
   745	        stagnation_cards = [
   746	            c.to_dict() for c in open_cards if c.type == "stagnation"
   747	        ]
   748	        profile_diff_cards = [
   749	            c.to_dict() for c in open_cards if c.type == "profile_diff"
   750	        ]
   751	
   752	        return {
   753	            "employee_id": employee_id,
   754	            "date": ref_today.isoformat(),
   755	            "reminders": reminders,
   756	            "stagnation_cards": stagnation_cards,
   757	            "profile_diff_cards": profile_diff_cards,
   758	        }

 # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 # Date Helpers (§14.7)
 # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 
 def get_today(demo_today: str | None = None) -> date:
     """Return the base date for stagnation and schedule calculations.
 
     Uses DEMO_TODAY environment variable (or argument) if set, otherwise UTC date.
     """
     raw = demo_today or os.environ.get("DEMO_TODAY")
     if raw:
         try:
             # Handle ISO date (YYYY-MM-DD) or timestamp
             if "T" in raw:
                 return datetime.fromisoformat(raw.replace("Z", "+00:00")).date()
             return date.fromisoformat(raw.strip())
         except (ValueError, TypeError):
             pass
     return datetime.now(timezone.utc).date()
 
 
@@ -268,106 +274,181 @@ If no meaningful knowledge is found, return null."""
             if not cleaned:
                 # Empty response is an API failure, not a deliberate null.
                 # Leave the mail unconsumed so the next sweep retries (R-2).
                 return EXTRACTION_FAILED
             data = json.loads(cleaned)
             if isinstance(data, dict) and "body_draft" in data:
                 key = data.get("item_key", "current_work")
                 body = data.get("body_draft", "").strip()
                 if body:
                     return key, body
             return None
         except Exception:
             # LLM call/parse failed. Do NOT copy mail text as a fallback (that
             # re-opens S-6) and do NOT consume the mail — retry next sweep (R-2).
             return EXTRACTION_FAILED
 
     # Heuristic extraction fallback (reached only when llm_client is None,
     # e.g. a local demo without GEMINI_API_KEY — never the default path, V-8/S-6).
     subj = mail.subject.strip()
     body = mail.body.strip()
     if len(body) > 10:
         return "current_work", f"{subj}: {body}"
     return None

 # Secretary Service Coordinator

 class SecretaryService:
     """Orchestrates secretary background sweep, morning digest, card CAS, and diff reviews."""
 
     def __init__(
         self,
         store: Store,
         kd_service: KnowledgeDiscoveryService,
         matching_engine: MatchingEngine | None = None,
         llm_client: Any | None = None,
         t1: float = STAGNATION_T1,
         t2: float = STAGNATION_T2,
+        connector: SourceConnector | None = None,
     ) -> None:
         self.store = store
         self.kd_service = kd_service
         self.matching_engine = matching_engine or kd_service.matching_engine
         self.llm_client = llm_client
         self.t1 = t1
         self.t2 = t2
+        self.connector = connector or SeedConnector()
         assert self.t1 < self.t2, f"t1 ({self.t1}) must be < t2 ({self.t2})"
 
+    def _sync_owners(
+        self, registered_agents: list[Any], profiles_list: list[Profile], ref_today: date
+    ) -> dict[str, int]:
+        """Sync-then-detect's "sync" half (§16.3): pull each tenant owner's
+        external data through `self.connector` and reconcile it via
+        `apply_fetch_result` before stagnation detection runs. `SeedConnector`
+        (the default) is a no-op, so this is a no-op in demo mode.
+
+        Single-owner mode (`GWS_SELF_EMPLOYEE_ID`): only that owner is
+        synced; every other owner is counted as skipped rather than synced.
+        A connector/reconciliation failure for one owner is caught and
+        counted so it never halts the sweep (§16.3 failure handling) — and
+        never logs task/mail titles or bodies.
+        """
+        today_str = ref_today.isoformat()
+        self_only = os.environ.get("GWS_SELF_EMPLOYEE_ID", "").strip()
+        target_owners = {a.employee_id for a in registered_agents} | {
+            p.employee_id for p in profiles_list
+        }
+
+        stats = {
+            "sync_tasks": 0,
+            "sync_schedules": 0,
+            "sync_mails": 0,
+            "sync_skipped": 0,
+            "sync_errors": 0,
+        }
+        for owner_id in sorted(target_owners):
+            if self_only and owner_id != self_only:
+                stats["sync_skipped"] += 1
+                continue
+            try:
+                fetch_result = self.connector.fetch(owner_id, today_str)
+                summary = apply_fetch_result(self.store, owner_id, fetch_result, today_str)
+            except Exception:
+                stats["sync_errors"] += 1
+                continue
+            stats["sync_tasks"] += summary.tasks
+            stats["sync_schedules"] += summary.schedules
+            stats["sync_mails"] += summary.mails
+            stats["sync_skipped"] += summary.skipped
+            stats["sync_errors"] += len(summary.errors)
+        return stats
+
+    def _apply_mail_retention(self, ref_today: date) -> None:
+        """Enforce mail_seed retention (§16.3 Gmail part D).
+
+        Runs every sweep regardless of connector: a mail whose body was
+        already cleared has nothing left to clear, and SeedConnector never
+        produces mail_seeds, so this is a no-op in demo mode.
+        - Any mail already `processed=True` with a non-empty body has its
+          body cleared (the diff proposal, if any, was already extracted
+          and written to a card; the raw body has no further use).
+        - Any mail (processed or not) older than MAIL_RETENTION_DAYS is
+          deleted outright.
+        """
+        for mail in self.store.list_mail_seeds():
+            received = _parse_date_or_timestamp(mail.received_at)
+            if received is not None and (ref_today - received).days >= MAIL_RETENTION_DAYS:
+                self.store.delete_mail_seed(mail.mail_id)
+                continue
+            if mail.processed and mail.body:
+                mail.body = ""
+                self.store.save_mail_seed(mail)
+
     def run_sweep(self, demo_today: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:

+        Sync-then-detect (§16.3): the tenant's owners (agents registered +

+        detection below then runs against whatever is in `Store` afterward
+        (seed data, previously-synced gws data, or freshly-synced gws data).
+

         2. Task status 'done' or score < T1: Transition open card to 'resolved'.

         4. score >= T2: Run preview search (public only, embedding_public). If candidates found,

     def get_morning_digest(
         self,
         employee_id: str,
         demo_today: str | None = None,
     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
         """Assemble dynamic morning digest for employee (§14.2, §14.8).
 
         Schedule rule order: Overdue -> Today -> Tomorrow -> Upcoming.
         Followed by open stagnation and profile_diff cards.
         """
         ref_today = get_today(demo_today)
         all_schedules = self.store.list_schedules(owner_employee_id=employee_id)
 
         # Categorize and order schedule reminders
         reminders: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
         for s in all_schedules:
             due_d = _parse_date_or_timestamp(s.due_date)
             if due_d is None:
                 category = "upcoming"
                 priority = 4
             elif due_d < ref_today:
                 category = "overdue"
                 priority = 1
             elif due_d == ref_today:
                 category = "today"
                 priority = 2
             elif (due_d - ref_today).days == 1:
                 category = "tomorrow"
                 priority = 3
             else:
                 category = "upcoming"
                 priority = 4
 
             reminders.append({
                 "item_id": s.item_id,
                 "kind": s.kind,
                 "title": s.title,
                 "due_date": s.due_date,
                 "due_category": category,

   891	class FakeSourceConnector(SourceConnector):
   892	    """Test double: `results` maps employee_id -> a FetchResult, or a
   893	    zero-arg callable returning one (so a test can change what the next
   894	    sweep sees). Missing owners get an empty complete FetchResult. Raises
   895	    if the mapped value is an Exception instance (to exercise the
   896	    per-owner failure path without stopping the whole sweep, §16.3).
   897	    """
   898	
   899	    def __init__(self, results: dict[str, object] | None = None) -> None:
   900	        self.results: dict[str, object] = results or {}
   901	        self.calls: list[str] = []
   902	
   903	    def fetch(self, owner_employee_id: str, today: str) -> FetchResult:
   904	        self.calls.append(owner_employee_id)
   905	        value = self.results.get(owner_employee_id, FetchResult(complete=True))
   906	        if isinstance(value, Exception):
   907	            raise value
   908	        if callable(value):
   909	            return value()
   910	        return value
   911

    50	    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(text.encode("utf-8")).decode("ascii").rstrip("=")

    54	    def __init__(self, status_code: int, data):
    55	        self.status_code = status_code
    56	        self._data = data

    62	class FakeSession:
    63	    """Routes `.get(url, params)` to a per-URL handler; records every call."""
    64	
    65	    def __init__(self, handlers: dict):
    66	        self._handlers = handlers
    67	        self.calls: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
    68	
    69	    def get(self, url: str, params=None):
    70	        params = dict(params or {})
    71	        self.calls.append((url, params))
    72	        handler = self._handlers.get(url)
    73	        if handler is None:
    74	            raise AssertionError(f"FakeSession: no handler registered for {url}")
    75	        return handler(params)
    76	
    77	
    78	def paged_handler(pages: list[tuple[list, bool]], items_key: str):
    79	    """pages[i] = (items, has_next_page). Pages are selected by sequential pageToken."""
    80	
    81	    def handler(params: dict):
    82	        token = params.get("pageToken")
    83	        idx = 0 if token is None else int(token)
    84	        items, has_next = pages[idx]
    85	        data = {items_key: items}
    86	        if has_next:
    87	            data["nextPageToken"] = str(idx + 1)
    88	        return FakeResponse(200, data)
    89	
    90	    return handler
    91	
    92	
    93	def failing_handler(status_code: int = 500):
    94	    def handler(params: dict):
    95	        return FakeResponse(status_code, {})
    96	
    97	    return handler

   114	    def setUp(self) -> None:
   115	        self._env_backup = dict(os.environ)
   116	        # Gmail is opt-in and off by default in most tests; individual tests turn it on.
   117	        os.environ.pop("GWS_GMAIL_ENABLED", None)
   118	
   119	    def tearDown(self) -> None:
   120	        os.environ.clear()
   121	        os.environ.update(self._env_backup)
   122	
   123	    def build_session(self, tasks_handlers=None, calendar_handler=None, gmail_handlers=None):
   124	        handlers = {}
   125	        handlers[f"{TASKS_BASE}/users/@me/lists"] = (tasks_handlers or {}).get(
   126	            "lists", paged_handler([([], False)], "items")
   127	        )
   128	        for list_id, handler in (tasks_handlers or {}).get("per_list", {}).items():
   129	            handlers[f"{TASKS_BASE}/lists/{list_id}/tasks"] = handler
   130	        handlers[f"{CALENDAR_BASE}/calendars/primary/events"] = calendar_handler or paged_handler(
   131	            [([], False)], "items"
   132	        )
   133	        if gmail_handlers:
   134	            if "labels" in gmail_handlers:
   135	                handlers[f"{GMAIL_BASE}/users/me/labels"] = gmail_handlers["labels"]
   136	            if "messages_list" in gmail_handlers:
   137	                handlers[f"{GMAIL_BASE}/users/me/messages"] = gmail_handlers["messages_list"]
   138	            for msg_id, handler in gmail_handlers.get("messages_get", {}).items():
   139	                handlers[f"{GMAIL_BASE}/users/me/messages/{msg_id}"] = handler
   140	        return FakeSession(handlers)
   141

   143	class TestTasksMapping(GwsConnectorTestBase):
   144	    def test_maps_fields_and_paginates_lists_and_tasks(self) -> None:
   145	        lists_handler = paged_handler(
   146	            [([{"id": "list1"}, {"id": "list2"}], False)], "items"
   147	        )
   148	        list1_tasks_handler = paged_handler(
   149	            [
   150	                (
   151	                    [
   152	                        {
   153	                            "id": "t1",
   154	                            "title": "Write report",
   155	                            "notes": "quarterly numbers",
   156	                            "due": "2026-08-25T00:00:00.000Z",
   157	                            "status": "needsAction",
   158	                            "updated": "2026-08-20T10:00:00.000Z",
   159	                        }
   160	                    ],
   161	                    True,
   162	                ),
   163	                (
   164	                    [
   165	                        {
   166	                            "id": "t2",
   167	                            "title": "Done task",
   168	                            "status": "completed",
   169	                            "updated": "2026-08-19T09:00:00.000Z",
   170	                        }
   171	                    ],
   172	                    False,
   173	                ),
   174	            ],
   175	            "items",
   176	        )
   177	        list2_tasks_handler = paged_handler([([], False)], "items")
   178	        session = self.build_session(
   179	            tasks_handlers={
   180	                "lists": lists_handler,
   181	                "per_list": {"list1": list1_tasks_handler, "list2": list2_tasks_handler},
   182	            }
   183	        )
   184	        connector = GoogleWorkspaceConnector(session)
   185	        result = connector.fetch("emp_jordan_lee", "2026-08-24")
   186	
   187	        self.assertTrue(result.complete)
   188	        self.assertEqual(result.errors, [])
   189	        by_id = {t.source_id: t for t in result.tasks}
   190	        self.assertEqual(
   191	            by_id["gws_task_list1_t1"].title, "Write report"
   192	        )
   193	        self.assertEqual(by_id["gws_task_list1_t1"].description, "quarterly numbers")
   194	        self.assertEqual(by_id["gws_task_list1_t1"].due_date, "2026-08-25")
   195	        self.assertEqual(by_id["gws_task_list1_t1"].status, "todo")
   196	        self.assertEqual(by_id["gws_task_list1_t1"].last_updated_at, "2026-08-20T10:00:00.000Z")
   197	        self.assertEqual(by_id["gws_task_list1_t2"].status, "done")
   198	        self.assertIsNone(by_id["gws_task_list1_t2"].due_date)
   199	
   200	    def test_no_due_date_maps_to_none(self) -> None:
   201	        lists_handler = paged_handler([([{"id": "list1"}], False)], "items")
   202	        tasks_handler = paged_handler(
   203	            [([{"id": "t1", "title": "No due", "status": "needsAction", "updated": "x"}], False)],
   204	            "items",
   205	        )
   206	        session = self.build_session(
   207	            tasks_handlers={"lists": lists_handler, "per_list": {"list1": tasks_handler}}
   208	        )
   209	        connector = GoogleWorkspaceConnector(session)
   210	        result = connector.fetch("emp_x", "2026-08-24")
   211	        self.assertIsNone(result.tasks[0].due_date)

   213	    def test_mid_pagination_failure_sets_complete_false_but_keeps_partial_results(self) -> None:
   214	        lists_handler = paged_handler([([{"id": "list1"}, {"id": "list2"}], False)], "items")
   215	
   216	        def list1_handler(params):
   217	            token = params.get("pageToken")
   218	            if token is None:
   219	                return FakeResponse(
   220	                    200,
   221	                    {
   222	                        "items": [
   223	                            {"id": "t1", "title": "Page one task", "status": "needsAction", "updated": "u1"}
   224	                        ],
   225	                        "nextPageToken": "1",
   226	                    },
   227	                )
   228	            return FakeResponse(500, {})
   229	
   230	        list2_handler = paged_handler(
   231	            [([{"id": "t2", "title": "List two task", "status": "needsAction", "updated": "u2"}], False)],
   232	            "items",
   233	        )
   234	        session = self.build_session(
   235	            tasks_handlers={
   236	                "lists": lists_handler,
   237	                "per_list": {"list1": list1_handler, "list2": list2_handler},
   238	            }
   239	        )
   240	        connector = GoogleWorkspaceConnector(session)
   241	        result = connector.fetch("emp_x", "2026-08-24")
   242	
   243	        self.assertFalse(result.complete)
   244	        self.assertTrue(any("tasks.list[list1]" in e for e in result.errors))
   245	        source_ids = {t.source_id for t in result.tasks}
   246	        # Partial data from the failed list's first page, plus the other list's full data.
   247	        self.assertIn("gws_task_list1_t1", source_ids)
   248	        self.assertIn("gws_task_list2_t2", source_ids)

   251	class TestCalendarMapping(GwsConnectorTestBase):
   252	    def test_window_classification_and_cancelled(self) -> None:
   253	        today = "2026-08-24"
   254	        events = [
   255	            {
   256	                "id": "ev_future",
   257	                "summary": "Future meeting",
   258	                "status": "confirmed",
   259	                "start": {"dateTime": "2026-08-25T09:00:00Z"},
   260	                "end": {"dateTime": "2026-08-25T10:00:00Z"},
   261	            },
   262	            {
   263	                "id": "ev_yesterday",
   264	                "summary": "Yesterday meeting",
   265	                "status": "confirmed",
   266	                "start": {"dateTime": "2026-08-23T09:00:00Z"},
   267	                "end": {"dateTime": "2026-08-23T10:00:00Z"},
   268	            },
   269	            {
   270	                "id": "ev_today_ended",
   271	                "summary": "Already-ended today meeting",
   272	                "status": "confirmed",
   273	                "start": {"dateTime": "2026-08-24T01:00:00Z"},
   274	                "end": {"dateTime": "2000-01-01T00:00:00Z"},  # far in the past -> ended
   275	            },
   276	            {
   277	                "id": "ev_today_upcoming",
   278	                "summary": "Not-yet-ended today meeting",
   279	                "status": "confirmed",
   280	                "start": {"dateTime": "2026-08-24T01:00:00Z"},
   281	                "end": {"dateTime": "2999-01-01T00:00:00Z"},  # far future -> not ended
   282	            },
   283	            {
   284	                "id": "ev_cancelled",
   285	                "summary": "Cancelled meeting",
   286	                "status": "cancelled",
   287	                "start": {"dateTime": "2026-08-25T09:00:00Z"},
   288	                "end": {"dateTime": "2026-08-25T10:00:00Z"},
   289	            },
   290	        ]
   291	        calendar_handler = paged_handler([(events, False)], "items")
   292	        session = self.build_session(calendar_handler=calendar_handler)
   293	        connector = GoogleWorkspaceConnector(session)
   294	        result = connector.fetch("emp_x", today)
   295	
   296	        self.assertTrue(result.complete)
   297	        by_id = {s.source_id: s for s in result.schedules}
   298	        self.assertEqual(by_id["gws_cal_ev_future_meeting_prep"].kind, "meeting_prep")
   299	        self.assertEqual(by_id["gws_cal_ev_yesterday_meeting_review"].kind, "meeting_review")
   300	        self.assertEqual(by_id["gws_cal_ev_today_ended_meeting_review"].kind, "meeting_review")
   301	        self.assertEqual(by_id["gws_cal_ev_today_upcoming_meeting_prep"].kind, "meeting_prep")
   302	        self.assertEqual(result.cancelled_ids, ["ev_cancelled"])
   303	        self.assertEqual(len(result.schedules), 4)
   304

   357	    def test_mapping_pagination_cap_and_truncation(self) -> None:
   358	        os.environ["GWS_GMAIL_ENABLED"] = "true"
   359	        os.environ["GWS_GMAIL_MAX_RESULTS"] = "3"
   360	        os.environ["GWS_MAIL_BODY_CHARS"] = "10"
   361	
   362	        labels_handler = paged_handler(
   363	            [([{"id": "Label_kd", "name": "kd-secretary"}], False)], "labels"
   364	        )
   365	
   366	        def messages_list_handler(params):
   367	            token = params.get("pageToken")
   368	            if token is None:
   369	                return FakeResponse(
   370	                    200, {"messages": [{"id": "m1"}, {"id": "m2"}], "nextPageToken": "1"}
   371	                )
   372	            # A 4th message exists upstream, but max_results=3 should cap us at m3.
   373	            return FakeResponse(200, {"messages": [{"id": "m3"}, {"id": "m4"}]})
   374	
   375	        long_subject = "S" * 120
   376	        long_body_plain = "This body is definitely longer than ten characters."
   377	
   378	        def make_message_handler(subject: str, body_text: str):
   379	            def handler(params):
   380	                return FakeResponse(
   381	                    200,
   382	                    {
   383	                        "internalDate": "1690000000000",
   384	                        "payload": {
   385	                            "mimeType": "multipart/alternative",
   386	                            "headers": [{"name": "Subject", "value": subject}],
   387	                            "parts": [
   388	                                {
   389	                                    "mimeType": "text/plain",
   390	                                    "body": {"data": _b64url(body_text)},
   391	                                },
   392	                                {
   393	                                    "mimeType": "text/html",
   394	                                    "body": {"data": _b64url(f"<p>{body_text}</p>")},
   395	                                },
   396	                            ],
   397	                        },
   398	                    },
   399	                )
   400	
   401	            return handler
   402	
   403	        session = self.build_session(
   404	            gmail_handlers={
   405	                "labels": labels_handler,
   406	                "messages_list": messages_list_handler,
   407	                "messages_get": {
   408	                    "m1": make_message_handler(long_subject, long_body_plain),
   409	                    "m2": make_message_handler("short", long_body_plain),
   410	                    "m3": make_message_handler("m3 subject", long_body_plain),
   411	                },
   412	            }
   413	        )
   414	        connector = GoogleWorkspaceConnector(session)
   415	        result = connector.fetch("emp_x", "2026-08-24")
   416	
   417	        self.assertTrue(result.complete)
   418	        self.assertEqual(len(result.mails), 3)  # capped at GWS_GMAIL_MAX_RESULTS
   419	        by_id = {m.source_id: m for m in result.mails}
   420	        self.assertIn("gws_mail_m1", by_id)
   421	        self.assertIn("gws_mail_m2", by_id)
   422	        self.assertIn("gws_mail_m3", by_id)
   423	        self.assertNotIn("gws_mail_m4", by_id)
   424	        self.assertEqual(len(by_id["gws_mail_m1"].subject), 80)
   425	        self.assertEqual(by_id["gws_mail_m1"].subject, long_subject[:80])
   426	        self.assertEqual(by_id["gws_mail_m1"].body, long_body_plain[:10])
   427	        self.assertEqual(len(by_id["gws_mail_m1"].body), 10)
   428	
   429	    def test_messages_get_failure_sets_complete_false(self) -> None:
   430	        os.environ["GWS_GMAIL_ENABLED"] = "true"
   431	        labels_handler = paged_handler(
   432	            [([{"id": "Label_kd", "name": "kd-secretary"}], False)], "labels"

   447	    def test_logs_never_contain_subject_or_body(self) -> None:
   448	        os.environ["GWS_GMAIL_ENABLED"] = "true"
   449	        secret_subject = "CONFIDENTIAL SUBJECT MARKER"
   450	        secret_body = "CONFIDENTIAL BODY MARKER"
   451	        labels_handler = paged_handler(
   452	            [([{"id": "Label_kd", "name": "kd-secretary"}], False)], "labels"
   453	        )
   454	        messages_list_handler = paged_handler([([{"id": "m1"}], False)], "messages")
   455	
   456	        def message_handler(params):
   457	            return FakeResponse(
   458	                200,
   459	                {
   460	                    "internalDate": "1690000000000",
   461	                    "payload": {
   462	                        "mimeType": "text/plain",
   463	                        "headers": [{"name": "Subject", "value": secret_subject}],
   464	                        "body": {"data": _b64url(secret_body)},
   465	                    },
   466	                },
   467	            )
   468	
   469	        session = self.build_session(
   470	            gmail_handlers={
   471	                "labels": labels_handler,
   472	                "messages_list": messages_list_handler,
   473	                "messages_get": {"m1": message_handler},
   474	            }
   475	        )
   476	        connector = GoogleWorkspaceConnector(session)
   477	        with self.assertLogs("knowledge_discovery.connectors.google_workspace", level="INFO") as cm:
   478	            result = connector.fetch("emp_x", "2026-08-24")
   479	        self.assertEqual(len(result.mails), 1)
   480	        combined_log_output = "\n".join(cm.output)
   481	        self.assertNotIn(secret_subject, combined_log_output)
   482	        self.assertNotIn(secret_body, combined_log_output)

   485	class TestDefaultSession(unittest.TestCase):
   486	    def test_default_session_uses_google_auth_default(self) -> None:
   487	        from knowledge_discovery.connectors import google_workspace
   488	
   489	        fake_credentials = object()
   490	        with mock.patch("google.auth.default", return_value=(fake_credentials, "proj")) as m_default, \
   491	            mock.patch("google.auth.transport.requests.AuthorizedSession") as m_session:
   492	            google_workspace.default_session()
   493	            m_default.assert_called_once()
   494	            called_scopes = m_default.call_args.kwargs.get("scopes")
   495	            self.assertIn(
   496	                "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/tasks.readonly", called_scopes
   497	            )
   498	            self.assertIn(
   499	                "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar.readonly", called_scopes
   500	            )
   501	            self.assertIn(
   502	                "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/gmail.readonly", called_scopes
   503	            )
   504	            m_session.assert_called_once_with(fake_credentials)

   507	class TestApplyFetchResult(unittest.TestCase):
   508	    """apply_fetch_result (C-2): field ownership, reconciliation, mail dedup."""
   509	
   510	    def setUp(self) -> None:
   511	        self.store = InMemoryStore()
   512	        self.owner = "emp_x"
   513	        self.today = "2026-08-24"
   514	
   515	    def test_first_sync_upserts_task_and_does_not_accrue_reschedule(self) -> None:
   516	        result = FetchResult(
   517	            tasks=[
   518	                TaskRecord(
   519	                    source_id="gws_task_l1_t1",
   520	                    title="Write report",
   521	                    description="notes",
   522	                    due_date="2026-08-25",
   523	                    status="todo",
   524	                    last_updated_at="2026-08-20T10:00:00Z",
   525	                )
   526	            ],
   527	            complete=True,
   528	        )
   529	        summary = apply_fetch_result(self.store, self.owner, result, self.today)
   530	        self.assertEqual(summary.tasks, 1)
   531	        self.assertEqual(summary.errors, [])
   532	        self.assertTrue(summary.complete)
   533	
   534	        task = self.store.get_task("gws_task_l1_t1")
   535	        self.assertIsNotNone(task)
   536	        self.assertEqual(task.owner_employee_id, self.owner)
   537	        self.assertEqual(task.title, "Write report")
   538	        self.assertEqual(task.due_date, "2026-08-25")
   539	        self.assertEqual(task.status, "todo")
   540	        self.assertEqual(task.source, "gws")

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# Knowledge Discovery — Tacit-Knowledge Connection Engine
     2	
     3	A multi-agent system where every employee's personal AI agent helps answer
     4	*"who in our company might know about this?"* — not by delivering a document,
     5	but by proposing a real human connection. Built for the All Things Agentic
     6	Hackathon (track: The Fortified Enterprise Fleet).
     7	
     8	**Core idea**: CRMs record accounts inside one business unit. The knowledge that
     9	crosses business units — "who has actually seen a clinic relocate before a
    10	sale?" — lives in people's heads and never lands in Salesforce, because it is
    11	made of confidences, relationships and case nuance. This system does
    12	*exploratory* matching over that tacit layer ("this person **might** know,
    13	given their background") and always resolves to a human connection: a 15-minute
    14	meeting proposal, or an honest decline with a reason and optional resources.
    15	
    16	## Architecture (governance three-in-one)
    17	
    18	| Concern | This implementation | Enterprise (GEAP) equivalent |
    19	|---|---|---|
    20	| Who exists (agent discovery) | `agents` registry (Firestore) | GEAP Agent Registry |
    21	| What may flow (control) | Schema registry + `supported_intents` checks in the transmission layer | Model Armor |
    22	| What did flow (audit) | `messages` collection + chat-style audit view | Agent Observability |
    23	
    24	Key properties:
    25	
    26	- **Candidate-isolated inference**: each candidate's agent sees ONLY the
    27	  question and its own profile (data boundary = process boundary).
    28	- **Private items**: things an owner deliberately keeps off the public profile.
    29	  Only the owner's agent knows them; when a question relates, the owner alone
    30	  gets a discreet ask. The audit log records that a private-based ask happened —
    31	  never its content. Masking is decided by the type system (item visibility +
    32	  content scan), not by the LLM's self-report.
    33	- **Honest non-matches**: the engine is allowed to say "no meaningful
    34	  connection" (deterministic vector floor OR LLM null), and dropped candidates
    35	  appear in the audit view with their reasons.
    36	
    37	Stack: Python / FastAPI on Cloud Run · Firestore (native) · Gemini 3.7 Flash +
    38	gemini-embedding-2 via **Vertex AI** · google-genai SDK.
    39	
    40	## Run locally
    41	
    42	```bash
    43	python3 -m venv .venv
    44	.venv/bin/pip install -r scripts/requirements.txt
    45	
    46	# Google Cloud auth (Firestore + Vertex AI)
    47	gcloud auth application-default login
    48	gcloud auth application-default set-quota-project <PROJECT_ID>
    49	
    50	# Seed 4 personas + 396 synthetic employees (embeddings via Vertex)
    51	GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI=true GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT=<PROJECT_ID> PYTHONPATH=src \
    52	  .venv/bin/python scripts/generate_seeds.py --use-firestore --project <PROJECT_ID> --embedder gemini
    53	
    54	# Serve
    55	USE_FIRESTORE=1 GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT=<PROJECT_ID> GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI=true \
    56	  DEMO_API_KEY=<your-demo-key> VECTOR_FLOOR=0.62 PYTHONPATH=src:. \
    57	  .venv/bin/uvicorn 'knowledge_discovery.server:create_app_from_env' --factory --port 8080
    58	```
    59	
    60	Open `http://localhost:8080/requester?api_key=<your-demo-key>` (also
    61	`/candidate`, `/audit`). All API and UI access requires the `api_key`.
    62	
    63	`VECTOR_FLOOR=0.62` is calibrated for gemini-embedding-2 (unrelated profiles
    64	score ~0.59 in that space; the default 0.20 suits only the offline test
    65	embedder).
    66	
    67	## Authentication (design v15 §16.1)
    68	
    69	`AUTH_MODE` selects how each request's `Principal` (`mode: demo|human|system`,
    70	`tenant_id`, `employee_id`, `email`) is resolved. Every route enforces a
    71	default-deny permission table over this Principal (design.md §16.1). Both
    72	modes below resolve `tenant_id` from the tenant ledger (see "Tenants" below,
    73	design.md §16.2) rather than a fixed constant.
    74	
    75	- **`AUTH_MODE=demo_key` (default)**: unchanged from the pre-Part-A behavior,
    76	  except the tenant is now whichever tenant's key matched. `X-API-Key` header
    77	  or `api_key` query parameter must match one tenant's key in the ledger;
    78	  the request resolves to `mode=demo` bound to that tenant, and each API
    79	  trusts the identity it is given in the request body/path (single shared
    80	  key *per tenant*, single actor plays all personas within it — see
    81	  "Demo-mode simplifications" below).
    82	- **`AUTH_MODE=iap`**: for Cloud Run behind Identity-Aware Proxy
    83	  (`--no-allow-unauthenticated`, IAP enabled on the service). Requests must
    84	  carry `X-Goog-IAP-JWT-Assertion`; it is verified with
    85	  `google.oauth2.id_token.verify_token` (ES256, IAP's public key endpoint,
    86	  `clock_skew_in_seconds=30`, issuer `https://cloud.google.com/iap`, `email`
    87	  claim required). The verified email resolves to a Principal via the tenant
    88	  ledger:
    89	  - email listed in some tenant's `system_accounts` (e.g. that tenant's
    90	    Scheduler job's OIDC identity) -> `mode=system`, bound to that tenant
    91	  - email's domain listed in some tenant's `email_domains`, and the email is
    92	    registered in that tenant's own `identities` collection (seeded by
    93	    `scripts/generate_seeds.py --database <tenant's database>`) -> `mode=human`,
    94	    `employee_id` resolved from that tenant's identity record
    95	  - otherwise -> `403`
    96	  - `IAP_AUDIENCE` (Cloud Run IAP format:
    97	    `/projects/<PROJECT_NUMBER>/locations/<REGION>/services/<SERVICE>`) is
    98	    required and format-checked at startup only when `AUTH_MODE=iap`. API
    99	    keys are not accepted in this mode — machine callers (Cloud Scheduler)
   100	    must also go through IAP via an OIDC token.
   101	  - The IAP public key is cached in-process (~1h, Cache-Control aware); a
   102	    failed refetch keeps serving the previous key for one more window before
   103	    failing closed with `401` (never a silent `503`).
   104	
   105	`GET /api/me` returns the resolved `{mode, tenant_id, employee_id}` and is
   106	used by `requester.html`/`candidate.html` to hide the demo persona switcher
   107	once `mode != demo`.
   108	
   109	## Tenants (design v15 §16.2, Part B)
   110	
   111	Each tenant is a separate Firestore **database** under the same GCP project —
   112	not a separate process, and not a query-time filter. A request only ever sees
   113	`ContextRouter.for_tenant(principal.tenant_id)`'s own `Store`; there is no
   114	`X-KD-Tenant` header, no all-tenant API key, and no code path that queries
   115	across tenants. **Blast radius**: a leaked tenant API key (or a compromised
   116	IAP identity bound to that tenant) exposes that tenant's data only — every
   117	other tenant is untouched. Firestore Security Rules are not part of this
   118	boundary (they don't apply to the server's own client); the boundary is
   119	"every route only ever asks for its own tenant's context, and there is no
   120	function that returns another tenant's".
   121	
   122	**Ledger** — env `TENANTS_JSON`, a JSON array; unset defaults to today's
   123	single-tenant setup (`meridian` / `(default)` / `meridian-care.example` /
   124	`DEMO_API_KEY`):
   125	
   126	```json
   127	[
   128	  {
   129	    "tenant_id": "meridian",
   130	    "database": "(default)",
   131	    "email_domains": ["meridian-care.example"],
   132	    "api_key_env": "DEMO_API_KEY",
   133	    "system_accounts": ["kd-scheduler-sa@<PROJECT_ID>.iam.gserviceaccount.com"]
   134	  },
   135	  {
   136	    "tenant_id": "acme",
   137	    "database": "kd-tenant-acme",
   138	    "email_domains": ["acme.example"],
   139	    "api_key": "literal-key-for-local-testing-only",
   140	    "system_accounts": []
   141	  }
   142	]
   143	```
   144	
   145	Each row needs either `api_key` (a literal value, local/testing only) or
   146	`api_key_env` (the name of an env var holding the key — the production way,
   147	so keys live in Secret Manager, not the ledger). Startup fails closed if
   148	`tenant_id`, `database`, any `email_domains` entry, or the resolved `api_key`
   149	repeats across tenants.
   150	
   151	- **API keys are per-tenant** (§16.2 C-42/W-2): `demo`/`system` principals are
   152	  bound to whichever tenant's key matched theirs; there is no key that spans
   153	  tenants. Cloud Scheduler jobs and the B-stage Agent Runtime are one set
   154	  *per tenant* (each Runtime's `KD_API_KEY` env holds that tenant's key).
   155	- **Process-lifetime cache**: `ContextRouter` builds each tenant's
   156	  Store/Service/Secretary lazily, on first request, and caches them for the
   157	  life of the process. Editing `TENANTS_JSON` only takes effect after a
   158	  restart (`gcloud run services update ... --update-env-vars` triggers one);
   159	  there is no live cache-invalidation endpoint.
   160	- **Second database (real Firestore) — create, seed, verify, delete**:
   161	
   162	  ```bash
   163	  # 1. create the database (one-time)
   164	  gcloud firestore databases create --database=kd-tenant-b --location=asia-northeast1 --type=firestore-native
   165	
   166	  # 2. seed it (identities included) via --database
   167	  GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI=true GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT=<PROJECT_ID> PYTHONPATH=src \
   168	    .venv/bin/python scripts/generate_seeds.py --use-firestore --project <PROJECT_ID> \
   169	    --database kd-tenant-b --embedder gemini
   170	
   171	  # 3. add the tenant to TENANTS_JSON, redeploy/restart the service, and confirm
   172	  #    with that tenant's key that /api/agents, /api/secretary/digest, etc. only
   173	  #    ever return kd-tenant-b's data (goal 25's real-Firestore smoke check)
   174	
   175	  # 4. delete the verification database when done
   176	  gcloud firestore databases delete --database=kd-tenant-b --quiet
   177	  ```
   178	
   179	## Tests
   180	
   181	```bash
   182	.venv/bin/python -m unittest discover -s tests   # ~200 tests, no network/credentials needed (13 B-stage tests skip without google-adk)
   183	```
   184	
   185	All external services (Firestore, Gemini) sit behind interfaces with in-memory
   186	/ deterministic fakes; the suite runs fully offline.
   187

   189	
   190	```bash
