   201	secret). Firestore is created in native mode via gcloud and has no client-SDK
   202	rules deployed — client SDK access is denied by default; every read/write goes
   203	through the server, which applies the requester/audit projections.
   204	
   205	## Secretary sweep trigger (Cloud Scheduler, design v9 §14.7 A-stage)
   206	
   207	The proactive secretary (`POST /api/secretary/sweep`) is fired daily by a Cloud
   208	Scheduler HTTP job that carries the demo API key as a header (the service stays
   209	`--allow-unauthenticated`; the endpoint itself returns 401 without the key).
   210	Production would use OIDC + a dedicated invoker SA (write-up future item).
   211	
   212	```bash
   213	gcloud scheduler jobs create http kd-secretary-sweep \
   214	  --location=asia-northeast1 --schedule="0 8 * * *" --time-zone=Asia/Tokyo \
   215	  --uri="https://<SERVICE_URL>/api/secretary/sweep" --http-method=POST \
   216	  --headers="X-API-Key=<DEMO_API_KEY>" --attempt-deadline=180s
   217	
   218	gcloud scheduler jobs run kd-secretary-sweep --location=asia-northeast1   # manual fire
   219	```
   220	
   221	## B-stage: secretary on GEAP Agent Runtime (design v11 §14.7)
   222	
   223	The secretary also runs as a first-class agent on **GEAP Agent Runtime** (Vertex AI
   224	Agent Engine, `reasoningEngines/4310793666370207744`, asia-northeast1). Two strictly
   225	separated entry points:
   226	
   227	- **Scheduled trigger = deterministic operation (no LLM).** `run_daily_sweep` is a
   228	  registered Agent Engine operation that calls Cloud Run `POST /api/secretary/sweep`
   229	  and returns its JSON. A non-2xx from Cloud Run raises, so the `:query` response is
   230	  non-2xx and Cloud Scheduler records a failure (failures are never silenced). The
   231	  A-stage job keeps running in parallel (sweep is idempotent) as a safety net.
   232	- **Dialogue = read-only LLM agent.** `LlmAgent` (Gemini 3.7 Flash, model endpoint
   233	  pinned to `global`) with a single tool `get_my_digest` that is scoped to the
   234	  session's own `user_id` (no employee_id argument). `run_daily_sweep` and every
   235	  write operation (confirm / dismiss / review) are deliberately **not** LLM tools, so
   236	  the Runtime secretary has no delivery authority.
   237	
   238	Detection, state machine, preview search and confirm all stay on Cloud Run; the
   239	Runtime agent only orchestrates and converses. Package: `src/secretary_agent/`
   240	(independent of `knowledge_discovery`; deps pinned in `scripts/requirements-agent.txt`).
   241	
   242	```bash
   243	# B-stage env (pinned deps) and tests (skip-0 here; skipped in the main venv)
   244	python3 -m venv .venv-agent && .venv-agent/bin/pip install -r scripts/requirements-agent.txt
   245	.venv-agent/bin/python -m unittest tests.test_secretary_agent
   246	
   247	# deploy / update (ADC; Reasoning Engine service agent needs secretAccessor on demo-api-key)
   248	GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT=<PROJECT_ID> .venv-agent/bin/python scripts/deploy_secretary_agent.py \
   249	  --project <PROJECT_ID> --location asia-northeast1 \
   250	  --staging-bucket gs://<PROJECT_ID>-agent-staging \
   251	  --api-base-url https://<SERVICE_URL> [--update projects/<N>/locations/asia-northeast1/reasoningEngines/<ID>]
   252	
   253	# scheduled trigger (OAuth as kd-scheduler-sa with roles/aiplatform.user; JSON body)
   254	gcloud scheduler jobs create http kd-secretary-sweep-runtime --location=asia-northeast1 \
   255	  --schedule="55 7 * * *" --time-zone=Asia/Tokyo --http-method=POST \
   256	  --uri="https://asia-northeast1-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/<PROJECT_ID>/locations/asia-northeast1/reasoningEngines/<ID>:query" \
   257	  --headers="Content-Type=application/json" --message-body='{"class_method":"run_daily_sweep","input":{}}' \
   258	  --oauth-service-account-email=kd-scheduler-sa@<PROJECT_ID>.iam.gserviceaccount.com \
   259	  --attempt-deadline=180s --max-retry-attempts=3
   260	
   261	# talk to the secretary as an employee (SDK)
   262	#   agent_engines.get(<resource>).stream_query(user_id="emp_jordan_lee", session_id=..., message="What's on my plate today?")
   263	```
   264	
   265	**Agent Registry.** The five Cloud Run agents (4 personal agents + A-stage secretary)
   266	and the Runtime secretary are registered in Agent Registry (asia-northeast1) as
   267	`no-spec` / `http-json` services with capability descriptions
   268	(`gcloud alpha agent-registry services create ...`, listed via
   269	`gcloud alpha agent-registry agents list --location=asia-northeast1`).
   270	
   271	Operational note (verified 2026-08-23): with default Runtime limits the replica restarted its
   272	workers on every request and Vertex answered `400 FAILED_PRECONDITION / Service Unavailable`
   273	to Cloud Scheduler even though the sweep had completed on Cloud Run. Two changes fixed it:
   274	`run_daily_sweep` is an async operation that runs the blocking HTTP call in a thread, and the
   275	deployment pins `resource_limits cpu=4/memory=8Gi`, `min_instances=1/max_instances=2`,
   276	`container_concurrency=4` (all set by `scripts/deploy_secretary_agent.py`). After that a
   277	Scheduler fire succeeds in one attempt (HTTP 200, one sweep). Avoid hammering the Runtime
   278	concurrently in tests; the production schedule fires once a day.
   279	
   280	Demo-mode notes: (1) the session `user_id` is asserted by the caller — anyone allowed to
   281	query the Runtime can name any user_id; the tool simply never lets the LLM pick a different
   282	employee (same single-key simplification as `/api/secretary/*`; production maps agent
   283	identity/IAM to employees). (2) Runtime Sessions keep each user_id's digest summaries;
   284	tracing is left disabled. (3) `kd-scheduler-sa` holds only a custom role with
   285	`aiplatform.reasoningEngines.query/get`.
   286	
   287	Teardown after the demo (stops all Runtime cost):
   288	
   289	```bash
   290	gcloud scheduler jobs delete kd-secretary-sweep-runtime --location=asia-northeast1 --quiet
   291	curl -X DELETE -H "Authorization: Bearer $(gcloud auth print-access-token)" \
   292	  "https://asia-northeast1-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/<PROJECT_ID>/locations/asia-northeast1/reasoningEngines/<ID>"
   293	```
   294	
   295	## Demo reset & recording-day procedure
   296	
   297	Seed dates are relative to a base date; the server evaluates "today" from
   298	`DEMO_TODAY` (ISO date, falls back to the real UTC date). To reset for a recording:
   299	
   300	```bash
   301	# 1. wipe everything (agents/profiles/messages/tasks/schedules/mail_seeds/cards) and reseed
   302	GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI=true GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT=<PROJECT_ID> GOOGLE_CLOUD_LOCATION=global PYTHONPATH=src \
   303	  .venv/bin/python scripts/generate_seeds.py --use-firestore --project <PROJECT_ID> \
   304	  --embedder gemini --clear --today YYYY-MM-DD
   305	
   306	# 2. pin the same date on the service
   307	gcloud run services update knowledge-discovery --region=asia-northeast1 --update-env-vars DEMO_TODAY=YYYY-MM-DD
   308	
   309	# 3. run one sweep (or wait for the Scheduler job) so the digest cards exist
   310	```
   311	
   312	## Demo-mode simplifications (deliberate)
   313	
   314	- Single shared demo API key; the candidate screen has a persona switcher so
   315	  one operator can play all four roles. Per-user auth is out of scope by spec.
   316	- `POST /api/probe/unregistered-intent` exists solely to demonstrate the
   317	  transmission-layer rejection (red row in the audit view) live.
   318	- `/api/secretary/*` (digest/confirm/dismiss/profile-diff review) does not
   319	  verify that the caller is the owning employee_id — it trusts the single
   320	  shared demo API key, same as `/api/query`'s existing `requester_id` param.
   321	  Anyone with the demo URL can read another persona's digest by swapping
   322	  `employee_id`. Out of scope by spec (M3, same posture as the persona
   323	  switcher above).

+## Authentication (design v15 §16.1)
+
+`AUTH_MODE` selects how each request's `Principal` (`mode: demo|human|system`,
+`tenant_id`, `employee_id`, `email`) is resolved. Every route enforces a
+default-deny permission table over this Principal (design.md §16.1). Both
+modes below resolve `tenant_id` from the tenant ledger (see "Tenants" below,
+design.md §16.2) rather than a fixed constant.
+
+- **`AUTH_MODE=demo_key` (default)**: unchanged from the pre-Part-A behavior,
+  except the tenant is now whichever tenant's key matched. `X-API-Key` header
+  or `api_key` query parameter must match one tenant's key in the ledger;
+  the request resolves to `mode=demo` bound to that tenant, and each API
+  trusts the identity it is given in the request body/path (single shared
+  key *per tenant*, single actor plays all personas within it — see
+  "Demo-mode simplifications" below).
+- **`AUTH_MODE=iap`**: for Cloud Run behind Identity-Aware Proxy
+  (`--no-allow-unauthenticated`, IAP enabled on the service). Requests must
+  carry `X-Goog-IAP-JWT-Assertion`; it is verified with
+  `google.oauth2.id_token.verify_token` (ES256, IAP's public key endpoint,
+  `clock_skew_in_seconds=30`, issuer `https://cloud.google.com/iap`, `email`
+  claim required). The verified email resolves to a Principal via the tenant
+  ledger:
+  - email listed in some tenant's `system_accounts` (e.g. that tenant's
+    Scheduler job's OIDC identity) -> `mode=system`, bound to that tenant
+  - email's domain listed in some tenant's `email_domains`, and the email is
+    registered in that tenant's own `identities` collection (seeded by
+    `scripts/generate_seeds.py --database <tenant's database>`) -> `mode=human`,
+    `employee_id` resolved from that tenant's identity record
+  - otherwise -> `403`
+  - `IAP_AUDIENCE` (Cloud Run IAP format:
+    `/projects/<PROJECT_NUMBER>/locations/<REGION>/services/<SERVICE>`) is
+    required and format-checked at startup only when `AUTH_MODE=iap`. API
+    keys are not accepted in this mode — machine callers (Cloud Scheduler)
+    must also go through IAP via an OIDC token.
+  - The IAP public key is cached in-process (~1h, Cache-Control aware); a
+    failed refetch keeps serving the previous key for one more window before
+    failing closed with `401` (never a silent `503`).
+
+`GET /api/me` returns the resolved `{mode, tenant_id, employee_id}` and is
+used by `requester.html`/`candidate.html` to hide the demo persona switcher
+once `mode != demo`.
+
+## Tenants (design v15 §16.2, Part B)
+
+Each tenant is a separate Firestore **database** under the same GCP project —
+not a separate process, and not a query-time filter. A request only ever sees
+`ContextRouter.for_tenant(principal.tenant_id)`'s own `Store`; there is no
+`X-KD-Tenant` header, no all-tenant API key, and no code path that queries
+across tenants. **Blast radius**: a leaked tenant API key (or a compromised
+IAP identity bound to that tenant) exposes that tenant's data only — every
+other tenant is untouched. Firestore Security Rules are not part of this
+boundary (they don't apply to the server's own client); the boundary is
+"every route only ever asks for its own tenant's context, and there is no
+function that returns another tenant's".
+
+**Ledger** — env `TENANTS_JSON`, a JSON array; unset defaults to today's
+single-tenant setup (`meridian` / `(default)` / `meridian-care.example` /
+`DEMO_API_KEY`):
+
+```json
+[
+  {
+    "tenant_id": "meridian",
+    "database": "(default)",
+    "email_domains": ["meridian-care.example"],
+    "api_key_env": "DEMO_API_KEY",
+    "system_accounts": ["kd-scheduler-sa@<PROJECT_ID>.iam.gserviceaccount.com"]
+  },
+  {
+    "tenant_id": "acme",
+    "database": "kd-tenant-acme",
+    "email_domains": ["acme.example"],
+    "api_key": "literal-key-for-local-testing-only",
+    "system_accounts": []
+  }
+]
+```
+
+Each row needs either `api_key` (a literal value, local/testing only) or
+`api_key_env` (the name of an env var holding the key — the production way,
+so keys live in Secret Manager, not the ledger). Startup fails closed if
+`tenant_id`, `database`, any `email_domains` entry, or the resolved `api_key`
+repeats across tenants.
+
+- **API keys are per-tenant** (§16.2 C-42/W-2): `demo`/`system` principals are
+  bound to whichever tenant's key matched theirs; there is no key that spans
+  tenants. Cloud Scheduler jobs and the B-stage Agent Runtime are one set
+  *per tenant* (each Runtime's `KD_API_KEY` env holds that tenant's key).
+- **Process-lifetime cache**: `ContextRouter` builds each tenant's
+  Store/Service/Secretary lazily, on first request, and caches them for the
+  life of the process. Editing `TENANTS_JSON` only takes effect after a
+  restart (`gcloud run services update ... --update-env-vars` triggers one);
+  there is no live cache-invalidation endpoint.
+- **Second database (real Firestore) — create, seed, verify, delete**:
+
+  ```bash
+  # 1. create the database (one-time)
+  gcloud firestore databases create --database=kd-tenant-b --location=asia-northeast1 --type=firestore-native
+
+  # 2. seed it (identities included) via --database
+  GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI=true GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT=<PROJECT_ID> PYTHONPATH=src \
+    .venv/bin/python scripts/generate_seeds.py --use-firestore --project <PROJECT_ID> \
+    --database kd-tenant-b --embedder gemini
+
+  # 3. add the tenant to TENANTS_JSON, redeploy/restart the service, and confirm
+  #    with that tenant's key that /api/agents, /api/secretary/digest, etc. only
+  #    ever return kd-tenant-b's data (goal 25's real-Firestore smoke check)
+
+  # 4. delete the verification database when done
+  gcloud firestore databases delete --database=kd-tenant-b --quiet
+  ```
+

 Usage (author ADC already logged in with tasks.readonly/calendar.readonly[/gmail.readonly]):
 
     PYTHONPATH=src .venv/bin/python scripts/gws_probe.py --owner emp_jordan_lee
 
 Prints counts, per-source completeness, and error strings only. Titles,
 descriptions, subjects, and bodies are never fetched-and-shown here — the
 connector's `fetch()` return value already withholds nothing but counts from
 this script by construction, but this script additionally never prints the
 record contents even though they are present in memory.
+
+`--apply-to-memory` additionally reconciles the fetch into a throwaway,
+empty `InMemoryStore` (via `apply_fetch_result`) and prints the resulting
+morning digest's counts by kind/tier only — still no titles or bodies.
 """
 
 from __future__ import annotations
 
 import argparse
 from datetime import datetime, timezone
 
+from knowledge_discovery.connectors.base import apply_fetch_result
 from knowledge_discovery.connectors.google_workspace import (
     GoogleWorkspaceConnector,
     default_session,
 )
 
 
 def main() -> int:
     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
     parser.add_argument("--owner", required=True, help="Probe target's employee_id")
     parser.add_argument(
         "--today",
         default=None,
         help="Reference date YYYY-MM-DD (default: current UTC date)",
     )
+    parser.add_argument(
+        "--apply-to-memory",
+        action="store_true",
+        help="Reconcile the fetch into an empty InMemoryStore and print digest counts by kind (no titles/bodies).",
+    )
     args = parser.parse_args()
 
     today = args.today or datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()
 
     session = default_session()
     connector = GoogleWorkspaceConnector(session)
     result = connector.fetch(args.owner, today)
 
     print(f"owner: {args.owner}")
     print(f"today: {today}")
     print(f"tasks: {len(result.tasks)}")
     print(f"schedules: {len(result.schedules)} (cancelled: {len(result.cancelled_ids)})")
     print(f"mails: {len(result.mails)}")
     print(f"complete: {result.complete}")
     print(f"errors: {len(result.errors)}")
     for error in result.errors:
         print(f"  - {error}")
+
+    if args.apply_to_memory:
+        from knowledge_discovery.store import InMemoryStore
+
+        store = InMemoryStore()
+        summary = apply_fetch_result(store, args.owner, result, today)
+        kinds: dict[str, int] = {}
+        for schedule in store.list_schedules(owner_employee_id=args.owner):
+            kinds[schedule.kind] = kinds.get(schedule.kind, 0) + 1
+        statuses: dict[str, int] = {}
+        for task in store.list_tasks(owner_employee_id=args.owner):
+            statuses[task.status] = statuses.get(task.status, 0) + 1
+        print("--- applied to empty InMemoryStore ---")
+        print(f"synced tasks: {summary.tasks} by status: {statuses}")
+        print(f"synced schedules: {summary.schedules} by kind: {kinds}")
+        print(f"synced mails: {summary.mails} (skipped: {summary.skipped})")
     return 0
 
 
 if __name__ == "__main__":
     raise SystemExit(main())

   238	17. （M3）監査画面に `stagnation_detected` / `preview_search` / `profile_diff_proposed` の行が「内容非表示」のマスク表示で現れ、タスク名・質問下書き・候補名が表示されないことを確認できる（fail-closedの適用確認）
   239	18. （M3・A段）Cloud Scheduler ジョブが構成され、`gcloud scheduler jobs run` の手動発火で sweep が実行される（Cloud Run ログに sweep 実行行が出る）こと、および `X-API-Key` ヘッダなしの `POST /api/secretary/sweep` が 401/403 で拒否されることを確認できる
   240	19. （M3・B段）Scheduler ジョブ `kd-secretary-sweep-runtime` を手動発火すると、Runtime の `run_daily_sweep` オペレーション（`:query`、LLM非介入）が Cloud Run の `/api/secretary/sweep` を呼び、Cloud Run ログに 200 が記録され Scheduler の試行が成功することを確認できる。さらに **Cloud Run を一時的に拒否させた状態（例: 無効なAPIキーで呼ぶ）で Runtime 応答が非2xxになり Scheduler が失敗として記録する**ことを確認できる（失敗の非無音化）。A段ジョブは有効のまま並走していることを確認できる
   241	20. （M3・B段）SDK経由で `async_stream_query(user_id="emp_jordan_lee", message="What's on my plate today?")` を送ると `get_my_digest` が（引数なし・セッションの user_id で）呼ばれ、Jordanの停滞カード・期日リマインドを含む要約がAI発言として返る＝**実モデル呼び出しが global エンドポイントで成功**することを確認できる。Runtime秘書のLLMツール一覧が `get_my_digest` のみであり、`run_daily_sweep` と書き込み系が**LLMツールとして存在しない**ことを確認できる。別の user_id のセッションから Jordan のダイジェストが読めないことを確認できる
   242	21. （M3・B段と独立）Agent Registry に、Cloud Run 上の4体エージェント（手動登録）と Runtime 秘書（自動登録）が**説明・能力情報つきで**一覧・検索できることを Console または API で確認できる。手動登録は 8/27 より前に完了している
   243	22. （M3・B段）(a) `secretary_agent` のツール関数・オペレーションの単体テストが HTTP フェイクでオフラインに通り、既存スイートが google-adk 未インストール環境でも壊れない（import失敗時 skipTest）こと、**および (b) ピン留め依存を入れた B段専用環境（`.venv-agent` 等）で同テストが skip 0件で通る**ことを確認できる（Y-5対応: 「ADKなしで壊れない」と「B段が動く」を別ゴールにする）
   244	23. （部品A）`AUTH_MODE=iap`: テスト用ES256鍵で署名した IAP 形式 JWT が実検証コードで employee_id に解決され、署名不正・aud/iss 不一致・期限切れ・未来iat・email欠落が 401、skew 境界（±30秒）が許容されること。human 主体で他人の資源を指す全列挙経路（query の requester_id／requester status／candidate asks／consent（他人宛 ask_audit_id・非pending・二重POST）／secretary digest／confirm・dismiss・review の card 所有者）が 403（二重POSTは 409）になること。`AUTH_MODE=demo_key` では demo 経路の機能結果が不変（2環境ゲート green）
   245	24. （部品A）`/api/me` が mode / tenant_id / employee_id を返し、requester.html が `human` のときペルソナ切替を隠すこと。権限表どおり human の sweep／probe が 403、system の human-facing 書き込みが 403 になること
   246	25. （部品B）2テナント（InMemory）で、テナントAのプロフィール・カード・監査行がテナントBの全APIから見えず、テナントAの鍵で system 主体がテナントBの digest を取れないこと。実Firestoreで第2DB `kd-tenant-b` にシードを入れ、テナント別に digest が分離されることを実機確認できる（確認後にDB削除）
   247	26. （部品B）テナント単位の鍵で sweep がそのテナントだけを処理すること。`IAP_AUDIENCE` の形式不正は iap モードでのみ起動失敗し、demo では不要なこと
   248	27. （部品C／D）`GoogleWorkspaceConnector` がフェイクAPI応答で tasks / schedules / mail_seeds（ラベル絞り込み・本文切り詰め・既存ID非再投入・処理後の本文消去・14日削除）に写像され、同じ入力の再同期で重複も `reschedule_count` 増加もなく、初回同期で加算されず、due 変更で +1、`last_updated_at` が Tasks.updated を写し、completed と取得結果からの消滅が `done`（→ `resolved`）、ページング途中エラー時に破壊的 reconciliation が行われず、Calendar の窓外・取消が削除されることをオフラインで確認できる
   249	28. （部品C／D）作者アカウントの読み取り専用資格情報（tasks/calendar/gmail.readonly）で `scripts/gws_probe.py` が件数を返し、シード無しの空 InMemory で `SOURCE_CONNECTOR=google_workspace GWS_SELF_EMPLOYEE_ID=…` の sweep→digest に実データ由来のリマインド／停滞カードが現れ、`GWS_GMAIL_ENABLED=true`＋ラベル付きメールで差分提案カードが生成されることを確認できる（記録は件数・種別のみ。本文は記録しない）

   251	30. （共通）降格した部品がある場合、spec FR25〜30・README・本ゴールが「部品のみ（実アダプタは将来）」に同時に書き換えられていること
   252	
   253	## 11. デモ動画の構成（3分・英語）
   254	
   255	1. **秘書の朝（〜30秒、v8で追加・冒頭シーン）**: 本人がUIを開く → モーニングダイジェスト（経費締切・週報・会議準備のリマインド）の**地続きに**停滞カード「Riverside Clinic移転のタスク、期日を2回延ばし5日止まっています。手がかりを持っていそうな人を探してあります」→ 候補＋理由を確認 → 質問下書きを一部編集して「依頼する」。ナレーション「質問はタイプするものではなく、秘書が先に気づくものになる」
   256	2. **幸福経路（〜85秒）**: （確定された質問から接続）監査画面でファネル「400件（スケール表示）→接点推論→配送3件・落選1件（理由付き）」 → 候補本人の画面に質問＋AI推定の候補理由 → 1体同意→MTG成立が双方に届く／1体辞退（資料添付）→依頼者に届く（§6-5の台本構成）
   257	3. **非公開項目打診（〜40秒）**: private項目を持つ候補への打診が本人にだけ届く → 監査画面では「内容非表示」の行 → 依頼者画面では通常候補と見分けがつかない → ナレーション「開示するかどうかは、本人だけが決める」
   258	4. **統制の3点セット（〜20秒）**: アーキ図カットで `agents` レジストリ（誰が居るか）＋スキーマレジストリ（何を流せるか）＋監査ログ（何が流れたか）＋トリガー（いつ動くか: Scheduler→将来はAgent Runtime）を示す。尺が余れば未登録型の拒否（赤表示）を実演
   259	
   260	秒数配分は目安（合計175秒＋タイトル・クロージング）。プレビュー無痕跡（候補者は確定まで何も知らない）は冒頭シーンのナレーションで一言添える（Fortified文脈での秘書の統制主張はこの1点に絞り、秘書機能自体を売りにしない——spec v7の位置づけ通り）。
   261

   260

   273	
   274	          return `

   228	    function updateAttachmentPlaceholder(auditId) {
   229	      const type = document.getElementById(`attType_${auditId}`).value;
   230	      const input = document.getElementById(`attContent_${auditId}`);
   231	      if (type === "link") {

   233	      } else if (type === "doc") {

   240	    async function giveConsent(auditId, decision) {
   241	      const agentId = document.getElementById("agentSelector").value;
   242	      let reasonText = "";
   243	      let attachment = null;
   244	
   245	      if (decision === "declined") {
   246	        reasonText = document.getElementById(`declineReason_${auditId}`)?.value.trim() || "Currently unable to participate.";
   247	        const attType = document.getElementById(`attType_${auditId}`)?.value;
   248	        const attContent = document.getElementById(`attContent_${auditId}`)?.value.trim();
   249	        if (attContent) {
   250	          attachment = { type: attType, content: attContent };
   251	        }
   252	      }
   253	
   254	      try {
   255	        const res = await fetch(`/api/candidate/${agentId}/consent`, {
   256	          method: "POST",
   257	          headers: {
   258	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   259	            "X-API-Key": API_KEY,
   260	          },
   261	          body: JSON.stringify({
   262	            ask_audit_id: auditId,
   263	            decision: decision,
   264	            reason_text: reasonText,
   265	            attachment: attachment
   266	          })
   267	        });
   268	
   269	        if (!res.ok) {
   270	          const errData = await res.json();
   271	          alert("Failed to submit consent decision: " + (errData.detail || "Unknown error"));
   272	          return;
   273	        }
   274	
   275	        await loadAsksForAgent();
   276	      } catch (err) {
   277	        alert("Connection error: " + err.message);
   278	      }
   279	    }

     1	"""Unit tests for knowledge_discovery.auth (design.md §16.1, Part A: FR25-26).
     2	
     3	Verifies:
     4	- IapResolver: real ES256-signed IAP-format JWTs are verified through the
     5	  actual google-auth verification code path (not hand-rolled crypto), with
     6	  negative cases for bad signature / aud / iss / expiry / future iat /
     7	  missing email, and the +/-30s clock-skew boundary.
     8	- The in-process cert cache: normal cache hits avoid network calls, a fetch
     9	  failure while the cache is still fresh is a non-event, and a fetch failure
    10	  once the cache is fully stale fails closed (raises, not silently 503s).
    11	- validate_iap_audience_format's Cloud Run IAP audience format check.
    12	- The full §16.1 permission table (demo/human/system x every route) and the
    13	  require_self ownership checks, driven over HTTP via TestClient with the
    14	  demo-key path unchanged and human/system driven by an injectable
    15	  PrincipalResolver test double.
    16	- The consent CAS (W-1): ask.to_entity/intent/pending verified atomically,
    17	  duplicate POST -> 409, mismatched ask -> 403.

    71	class _FakeResponse:
    72	    def __init__(self, status: int, data: bytes, headers: dict | None = None) -> None:
    73	        self.status = status
    74	        self.data = data
    75	        self.headers = headers or {}
    76	
    77	
    78	class _FakeCertsTransport:
    79	    """Stands in for google.auth.transport.Request: a callable(url, method=...) -> response."""
    80	
    81	    def __init__(self, certs: dict, status: int = 200, headers: dict | None = None, raise_exc: Exception | None = None) -> None:
    82	        self.certs = certs
    83	        self.status = status
    84	        self.headers = headers or {}
    85	        self.raise_exc = raise_exc
    86	        self.call_count = 0
    87	
    88	    def __call__(self, url: str, method: str = "GET", **kwargs) -> _FakeResponse:
    89	        self.call_count += 1
    90	        if self.raise_exc is not None:
    91	            raise self.raise_exc
    92	        import json
    93	
    94	        return _FakeResponse(self.status, json.dumps(self.certs).encode("utf-8"), self.headers)
    95	
    96	
    97	class _FakeRequest:
    98	    """Minimal stand-in for fastapi.Request: only .headers is used by resolvers."""
    99	
   100	    def __init__(self, headers: dict | None = None, query_params: dict | None = None) -> None:
   101	        self.headers = headers or {}
   102	        self.query_params = query_params or {}
   103	
   104	
   105	class _FixedPrincipalResolver:
   106	    """Test double: always resolves to whatever Principal is currently set,
   107	    regardless of the incoming request. Lets HTTP-level permission-table
   108	    tests flip principal.mode/employee_id per assertion without needing to
   109	    forge distinct credentials for demo/human/system on every call."""
   110	
   111	    def __init__(self, principal: "Principal") -> None:
   112	        self.principal = principal
   113	
   114	    def resolve(self, request) -> "Principal":
   115	        return self.principal
   116	
   117	
   118	def _generate_ec_keypair():
   119	    priv = ec.generate_private_key(ec.SECP256R1())
   120	    pub_pem = priv.public_key().public_bytes(
   121	        encoding=serialization.Encoding.PEM,
   122	        format=serialization.PublicFormat.SubjectPublicKeyInfo,
   123	    ).decode("utf-8")
   124	    return priv, pub_pem
   125	
   126	
   127	def _make_iap_jwt(priv, kid: str = "test-kid-1", **claim_overrides) -> str:
   128	    now = int(time.time())
   129	    payload = {
   130	        "iss": IAP_ISSUER,
   131	        "aud": TEST_AUDIENCE,
   132	        "iat": now,
   133	        "exp": now + 600,
   134	        "email": "rachel.kim@meridian-care.example",
   135	        "sub": "accounts.google.com:1234567890",
   136	    }
   137	    payload.update(claim_overrides)
   138	    signer = es256.ES256Signer(priv, key_id=kid)
   139	    return google_jwt.encode(signer, payload, header={"alg": "ES256", "kid": kid})
   140

   142	@unittest.skipUnless(HAS_CRYPTO and HAS_FASTAPI, "cryptography/google-auth/fastapi not available")
   143	class TestIapJwtVerification(unittest.TestCase):
   144	    """IAP assertion verification through the real google-auth code path."""
   145	
   146	    def setUp(self) -> None:
   147	        self.priv, self.pub_pem = _generate_ec_keypair()
   148	        self.other_priv, self.other_pub_pem = _generate_ec_keypair()
   149	        self.certs = {"test-kid-1": self.pub_pem}
   150	        self.transport = _FakeCertsTransport(self.certs)
   151	        self.cert_cache = _CachingCertsRequest()
   152	        self.cert_cache._transport = self.transport
   153	        self.store = InMemoryStore()
   154	        self.store.save_identity("rachel.kim@meridian-care.example", "emp_rachel_kim")
   155	        self.registry = TenantRegistry.single(
   156	            tenant_id="meridian",
   157	            email_domains=["meridian-care.example"],
   158	            api_key="unused-in-iap-mode",
   159	            system_accounts=["kd-sweeper@my-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com"],
   160	        )
   161	        self.router = ContextRouter(
   162	            self.registry,
   163	            context_factory=lambda tenant: TenantContext(tenant=tenant, store=self.store),
   164	        )
   165	        self.resolver = IapResolver(
   166	            registry=self.registry,
   167	            router=self.router,
   168	            audience=TEST_AUDIENCE,
   169	            cert_cache=self.cert_cache,
   170	        )
   171	
   172	    def _resolve(self, token: str) -> Principal:
   173	        request = _FakeRequest(headers={"x-goog-iap-jwt-assertion": token})
   174	        return self.resolver.resolve(request)

   176	    def test_valid_human_assertion_resolves_employee_id(self) -> None:
   177	        token = _make_iap_jwt(self.priv)
   178	        principal = self._resolve(token)
   179	        self.assertEqual(principal.mode, "human")
   180	        self.assertEqual(principal.employee_id, "emp_rachel_kim")
   181	        self.assertEqual(principal.email, "rachel.kim@meridian-care.example")
   182	        self.assertEqual(principal.tenant_id, "meridian")
   183	
   184	    def test_email_case_is_normalized(self) -> None:
   185	        token = _make_iap_jwt(self.priv, email="Rachel.Kim@Meridian-Care.EXAMPLE")
   186	        principal = self._resolve(token)
   187	        self.assertEqual(principal.mode, "human")
   188	        self.assertEqual(principal.employee_id, "emp_rachel_kim")

   190	    def test_system_account_resolves_system_mode(self) -> None:

   192	        principal = self._resolve(token)
   193	        self.assertEqual(principal.mode, "system")
   194	        self.assertIsNone(principal.employee_id)

   196	    def test_missing_header_is_401(self) -> None:
   197	        request = _FakeRequest(headers={})
   198	        with self.assertRaises(Exception) as ctx:
   199	            self.resolver.resolve(request)
   200	        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.status_code, 401)
   201	
   202	    def test_bad_signature_is_401(self) -> None:
   203	        # kid matches but the certs mapping holds a DIFFERENT key's public cert.
   204	        bad_certs = {"test-kid-1": self.other_pub_pem}
   205	        self.cert_cache._cached_response = None  # force refetch with bad certs
   206	        self.transport.certs = bad_certs
   207	        token = _make_iap_jwt(self.priv)
   208	        with self.assertRaises(Exception) as ctx:
   209	            self._resolve(token)
   210	        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.status_code, 401)
   211	
   212	    def test_audience_mismatch_is_401(self) -> None:
   213	        token = _make_iap_jwt(self.priv, aud="/projects/999/locations/us-central1/services/other-svc")
   214	        with self.assertRaises(Exception) as ctx:
   215	            self._resolve(token)
   216	        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.status_code, 401)
   217	
   218	    def test_issuer_mismatch_is_401(self) -> None:
   219	        token = _make_iap_jwt(self.priv, iss="https://not-iap.example.com")
   220	        with self.assertRaises(Exception) as ctx:
   221	            self._resolve(token)
   222	        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.status_code, 401)
   223	
   224	    def test_expired_token_is_401(self) -> None:
   225	        now = int(time.time())
   226	        token = _make_iap_jwt(self.priv, iat=now - 3600, exp=now - 1800)
   227	        with self.assertRaises(Exception) as ctx:
   228	            self._resolve(token)
   229	        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.status_code, 401)
   230	
   231	    def test_future_iat_is_401(self) -> None:
   232	        now = int(time.time())
   233	        token = _make_iap_jwt(self.priv, iat=now + 3600, exp=now + 4200)
   234	        with self.assertRaises(Exception) as ctx:
   235	            self._resolve(token)
   236	        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.status_code, 401)
   237	
   238	    def test_missing_email_claim_is_401(self) -> None:
   239	        now = int(time.time())
   240	        payload_overrides = {"iat": now, "exp": now + 600}
   241	        signer = es256.ES256Signer(self.priv, key_id="test-kid-1")
   242	        payload = {
   243	            "iss": IAP_ISSUER,
   244	            "aud": TEST_AUDIENCE,
   245	            "sub": "accounts.google.com:1",
   246	            **payload_overrides,
   247	        }
   248	        token = google_jwt.encode(signer, payload, header={"alg": "ES256", "kid": "test-kid-1"})
   249	        with self.assertRaises(Exception) as ctx:
   250	            self._resolve(token)
   251	        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.status_code, 401)
   252	
   253	    def test_clock_skew_boundary_accepts_within_30s(self) -> None:
   254	        now = int(time.time())
   255	        # exp exactly 30s in the past: latest = exp + skew(30) == now -> accepted.
   256	        token = _make_iap_jwt(self.priv, iat=now - 630, exp=now - 30)
   257	        principal = self._resolve(token)
   258	        self.assertEqual(principal.mode, "human")
   259	
   260	    def test_clock_skew_boundary_rejects_beyond_30s(self) -> None:
   261	        now = int(time.time())
   262	        # exp 31s in the past: latest = exp + skew(30) == now - 1 < now -> rejected.
   263	        token = _make_iap_jwt(self.priv, iat=now - 631, exp=now - 31)
   264	        with self.assertRaises(Exception) as ctx:
   265	            self._resolve(token)
   266	        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.status_code, 401)
   267	
   268	    def test_unregistered_domain_is_403(self) -> None:
   269	        token = _make_iap_jwt(self.priv, email="someone@not-meridian.example")
   270	        with self.assertRaises(Exception) as ctx:
   271	            self._resolve(token)
   272	        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.status_code, 403)
   273	
   274	    def test_domain_registered_but_no_identity_is_403(self) -> None:
   275	        token = _make_iap_jwt(self.priv, email="ghost.person@meridian-care.example")
   276	        with self.assertRaises(Exception) as ctx:
   277	            self._resolve(token)
   278	        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.status_code, 403)

   281	@unittest.skipUnless(HAS_CRYPTO and HAS_FASTAPI, "cryptography/google-auth/fastapi not available")
   282	class TestCertCache(unittest.TestCase):
   283	    """Cert cache resilience: cache hits skip the network; a failed refetch
   284	    serves stale certs through a grace window, then fails closed (401)."""
   285	
   286	    def setUp(self) -> None:
   287	        self.priv, self.pub_pem = _generate_ec_keypair()
   288	        self.certs = {"test-kid-1": self.pub_pem}
   289	
   290	    def test_cache_hit_skips_network(self) -> None:
   291	        transport = _FakeCertsTransport(self.certs)
   292	        cache = _CachingCertsRequest(default_ttl_seconds=60)
   293	        cache._transport = transport
   294	        cache(url="https://www.gstatic.com/iap/verify/public_key")
   295	        cache(url="https://www.gstatic.com/iap/verify/public_key")
   296	        self.assertEqual(transport.call_count, 1)
   297	
   298	    def test_fetch_failure_within_grace_serves_stale(self) -> None:
   299	        transport = _FakeCertsTransport(self.certs)
   300	        cache = _CachingCertsRequest(default_ttl_seconds=0.05)
   301	        cache._transport = transport
   302	        first = cache(url="u")
   303	        time.sleep(0.08)  # past ttl, still within the 2x grace window
   304	        transport.raise_exc = RuntimeError("network down")
   305	        second = cache(url="u")
   306	        self.assertIs(first, second)
   307	
   308	    def test_fetch_failure_beyond_grace_raises(self) -> None:
   309	        transport = _FakeCertsTransport(self.certs)
   310	        cache = _CachingCertsRequest(default_ttl_seconds=0.05)
   311	        cache._transport = transport
   312	        cache(url="u")
   313	        transport.raise_exc = RuntimeError("network down")
   314	        time.sleep(0.15)  # past ttl*2 grace window entirely
   315	        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
   316	            cache(url="u")
   317

   334	class TestPermissionTable(unittest.TestCase):
   335	    """§16.1 permission table across demo / human / system, and require_self."""
   336	
   337	    def setUp(self) -> None:
   338	        self.store = InMemoryStore()
   339	        self.inferencer = FakeConnectionInferencer()
   340	        self.matching_engine = MatchingEngine(
   341	            inferencer=self.inferencer,
   342	            vector_floor=0.20,
   343	            connection_threshold=0.50,
   344	            max_dispatch_k=3,
   345	            funnel_limit=20,
   346	        )
   347	        self.service = KnowledgeDiscoveryService(store=self.store, matching_engine=self.matching_engine)
   348	
   349	        self.agent_marcus = Agent(
   350	            agent_id="agent_marcus_delgado",
   351	            employee_id="emp_marcus_delgado",

   353	            supported_intents=["connect_ask", "connect_ask_private", "no_connection"],
   354	            active=True,
   355	        )
   356	        self.profile_marcus = Profile(
   357	            employee_id="emp_marcus_delgado",

   359	            role="Commercial Broker",

   361	        )
   362	        self.agent_rachel = Agent(
   363	            agent_id="agent_rachel_kim",

   374	        )
   375	        self.store.save_agent(self.agent_marcus)
   376	        self.store.save_agent(self.agent_rachel)
   377	        self.store.save_profile(self.profile_marcus)
   378	        self.store.save_profile(self.profile_rachel)
   379

   388	    def _as(self, mode: str, employee_id: str | None = None) -> None:
   389	        self.principal_resolver.principal = Principal(
   390	            mode=mode,
   391	            tenant_id="meridian",
   392	            employee_id=employee_id,
   393	            email=f"{employee_id}@meridian-care.example" if employee_id else None,
   394	        )

   396	    def _make_stagnation_card(self, owner_employee_id: str) -> str:
   397	        card = Card(
   398	            card_id=f"card_test_{owner_employee_id}_{int(time.time() * 1000)}",
   399	            owner_employee_id=owner_employee_id,
   400	            type="stagnation",
   401	            tier="request_draft",
   402	            payload={"task_id": "task_x", "task_title": "Test task"},
   403	            status="open",
   404	        )
   405	        self.store.save_card(card)
   406	        return card.card_id

   694	            employee_id="emp_rachel_kim",

   696	            role="Account Manager",

   708	                cited_item_keys=["current_work"],

     1	"""Unit tests for knowledge_discovery.tenancy (design.md §16.2, FR27, Part B).
     2	
     3	Verifies:
     4	- TenantRegistry startup validation: duplicate tenant_id / database /
     5	  email_domain / api_key all fail to construct (§16.2 "起動時検証").
     6	- TenantRegistry.from_env() defaults to the single current-compatible tenant
     7	  ("meridian" / "(default)" / "meridian-care.example" / DEMO_API_KEY) when
     8	  TENANTS_JSON is unset.
     9	- End-to-end, over HTTP (TestClient), that two tenants driven by two
    10	  InMemoryStores through server.create_app are fully isolated: tenant A's
    11	  agents/profiles/messages/cards never appear through tenant B's API key,
    12	  across every route (agents / query / requester status / candidate asks /
    13	  secretary digest / audit / sweep), including when the same employee_id and
    14	  agent_id happen to exist in both tenants.
    15	- A tenant-scoped API key resolves system-mode digest requests to its own
    16	  tenant only (no cross-tenant employee_id lookup).
    17	- A tenant-scoped sweep processes only that tenant's tasks.

    57	class TestTenantRegistryValidation(unittest.TestCase):
    58	    def test_duplicate_tenant_id_fails(self) -> None:
    59	        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
    60	            TenantRegistry(
    61	                [
    62	                    TenantConfig("t1", "db1", ("a.example",), "key1"),
    63	                    TenantConfig("t1", "db2", ("b.example",), "key2"),
    64	                ]
    65	            )
    66	
    67	    def test_duplicate_database_fails(self) -> None:
    68	        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
    69	            TenantRegistry(
    70	                [
    71	                    TenantConfig("t1", "shared-db", ("a.example",), "key1"),
    72	                    TenantConfig("t2", "shared-db", ("b.example",), "key2"),
    73	                ]
    74	            )
    75	
    76	    def test_duplicate_email_domain_fails(self) -> None:
    77	        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
    78	            TenantRegistry(
    79	                [
    80	                    TenantConfig("t1", "db1", ("shared.example",), "key1"),
    81	                    TenantConfig("t2", "db2", ("shared.example",), "key2"),
    82	                ]
    83	            )
    84	
    85	    def test_duplicate_api_key_fails(self) -> None:
    86	        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
    87	            TenantRegistry(
    88	                [
    89	                    TenantConfig("t1", "db1", ("a.example",), "shared-key"),
    90	                    TenantConfig("t2", "db2", ("b.example",), "shared-key"),
    91	                ]
    92	            )
    93	
    94	    def test_empty_registry_fails(self) -> None:
    95	        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
    96	            TenantRegistry([])
    97	
    98	    def test_valid_two_tenant_registry_resolves(self) -> None:
    99	        registry = TenantRegistry(
   100	            [
   101	                TenantConfig("t1", "db1", ("a.example",), "key1", system_accounts=("sa1@x.example",)),
   102	                TenantConfig("t2", "db2", ("b.example",), "key2", system_accounts=("sa2@x.example",)),
   103	            ]
   104	        )
   105	        self.assertEqual(registry.resolve_by_api_key("key1").tenant_id, "t1")
   106	        self.assertEqual(registry.resolve_by_api_key("key2").tenant_id, "t2")
   107	        self.assertIsNone(registry.resolve_by_api_key("no-such-key"))
   108	        self.assertEqual(registry.resolve_by_email_domain("a.example").tenant_id, "t1")
   109	        self.assertEqual(registry.resolve_by_system_account("sa2@x.example").tenant_id, "t2")

   112	class TestTenantRegistryFromEnv(unittest.TestCase):
   113	    def test_default_is_single_current_compatible_tenant(self) -> None:
   114	        """TENANTS_JSON unset -> exactly today's behavior (§16.2 e)."""
   115	        registry = TenantRegistry.from_env(env={})
   116	        self.assertEqual(len(registry.tenants), 1)
   117	        tenant = registry.tenants[0]
   118	        self.assertEqual(tenant.tenant_id, DEFAULT_TENANT_ID)
   119	        self.assertEqual(tenant.database, DEFAULT_DATABASE)
   120	        self.assertEqual(tenant.email_domains, DEFAULT_EMAIL_DOMAINS)
   121	        self.assertEqual(tenant.api_key, DEFAULT_API_KEY_FALLBACK)
   122	
   123	    def test_default_honors_demo_api_key_env(self) -> None:
   124	        registry = TenantRegistry.from_env(env={"DEMO_API_KEY": "my-custom-key"})
   125	        self.assertEqual(registry.tenants[0].api_key, "my-custom-key")
   126	
   127	    def test_tenants_json_builds_multi_tenant_registry(self) -> None:
   128	        env = {
   129	            "TENANTS_JSON": (
   130	                '[{"tenant_id":"acme","database":"acme-db","email_domains":["acme.example"],'
   131	                '"api_key":"acme-key","system_accounts":["sa@acme.example"]},'
   132	                '{"tenant_id":"globex","database":"globex-db","email_domains":["globex.example"],'
   133	                '"api_key_env":"GLOBEX_KEY"}]'
   134	            ),
   135	            "GLOBEX_KEY": "globex-key-from-env",
   136	        }
   137	        registry = TenantRegistry.from_env(env=env)
   138	        self.assertEqual(len(registry.tenants), 2)
   139	        self.assertEqual(registry.resolve_by_api_key("acme-key").tenant_id, "acme")
   140	        self.assertEqual(registry.resolve_by_api_key("globex-key-from-env").tenant_id, "globex")

   143	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   144	# End-to-end tenant isolation over HTTP
   145	# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   146	
   147	
   148	class _KeyedSystemPrincipalResolver:
   149	    """Test double: same API-key -> tenant binding as DemoKeyResolver, but
   150	    resolves to mode='system' instead of mode='demo'. Used to check
   151	    system-mode digest isolation without re-exercising IapResolver's JWT
   152	    verification machinery (already covered end-to-end by test_auth.py)."""
   153	
   154	    def __init__(self, registry: TenantRegistry) -> None:
   155	        self.registry = registry
   156	
   157	    def resolve(self, request):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
   158	        key = request.headers.get("x-api-key") or request.query_params.get("api_key")
   159	        tenant = self.registry.resolve_by_api_key(key) if key else None
   160	        if tenant is None:
   161	            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="bad key")
   162	        return Principal(mode="system", tenant_id=tenant.tenant_id, employee_id=None, email=None)
   163	
   164	
   165	@unittest.skipUnless(HAS_FASTAPI, "fastapi is not installed")
   166	class TestTenantIsolation(unittest.TestCase):
   167	    """Two tenants, both InMemory, sharing the SAME agent_id/employee_id, to
   168	    make sure isolation holds even when IDs collide across tenants -- a
   169	    routing bug would leak tenant A's data through tenant B's identical IDs."""
   170	
   171	    def setUp(self) -> None:
   172	        self.store_a = InMemoryStore()
   173	        self.store_b = InMemoryStore()
   174	
   175	        for label, store in (("A", self.store_a), ("B", self.store_b)):
   176	            store.save_agent(
   177	                Agent(
   178	                    agent_id="agent_shared",
   179	                    employee_id="emp_shared",
   180	                    display_name=f"Tenant {label} Agent",

     1	"""Source connector interface (design.md §16.3, FR28〜30).
     2	
     3	`SourceConnector.fetch()` is the data-source differentiation point: it takes an
     4	owner and today's date and returns a `FetchResult` describing what that owner's
     5	external tools currently look like. It has no side effects — it never touches
     6	`Store`. Reconciling a `FetchResult` against `Store` (upsert, destructive
     7	delete under the completeness barrier, `reschedule_count` bookkeeping, etc.) is
     8	`apply_fetch_result`'s job, implemented in the follow-up integration step (C-2)
     9	once `models.py` gains `Task.source` / `Task.last_seen_due` / `Schedule.source`
    10	and `Store` gains identity/deletion/filter APIs. This module only fixes that
    11	function's signature and contract so C-2 has a stable target to implement
    12	against.
    13	"""
    14	
    15	from __future__ import annotations
    16	
    17	import abc
    18	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    19	from typing import Any
    20	
    21	
    22	@dataclass
    23	class TaskRecord:
    24	    """One task as reported by a connector, pre-write to the `Task` model.
    25	
    26	    Only the fields a connector owns (§16.3「フィールドの所有」) live here:
    27	    title / description / due_date / status / last_updated_at / source_id.
    28	    `reschedule_count`, `created_at`, and `status_changed_at` are the
    29	    secretary's own bookkeeping and are computed by `apply_fetch_result`
    30	    against the previously stored record — a connector never sees them.
    31	
    32	    Attributes:
    33	        source_id: Stable external key (e.g. `gws_task_<listId>_<taskId>`),
    34	            used as the `Task.task_id` when applied.
    35	        title: Task title.
    36	        description: Task notes/description.
    37	        due_date: Due date normalized to UTC `YYYY-MM-DD`, or None if unset.
    38	        status: `"todo"` or `"done"` (connector-side; `"in_progress"` is a
    39	            secretary/user-only state and never produced by a connector).
    40	        last_updated_at: ISO8601 timestamp the source reports as its own
    41	            last-modified time (e.g. Tasks API `updated`).
    42	    """
    43	
    44	    source_id: str
    45	    title: str
    46	    description: str = ""
    47	    due_date: str | None = None
    48	    status: str = "todo"
    49	    last_updated_at: str = ""
    50	
    51	
    52	@dataclass
    53	class ScheduleRecord:
    54	    """One schedule reminder as reported by a connector.
    55	
    56	    Attributes:
    57	        source_id: Stable external key (e.g. `gws_cal_<eventId>_<kind>`),
    58	            used as the `Schedule.item_id` when applied.
    59	        kind: `"meeting_prep"` | `"meeting_review"` (the only kinds a
    60	            connector produces; `expense_deadline` / `weekly_report` /
    61	            `monthly_report` / `journal` stay seed/rule-driven per §16.3).
    62	        title: Schedule title (e.g. the calendar event summary).
    63	        due_date: Due date in `YYYY-MM-DD`.
    64	    """
    65	
    66	    source_id: str
    67	    kind: str
    68	    title: str
    69	    due_date: str
    70	
    71	
    72	@dataclass
    73	class MailRecord:
    74	    """One mail seed as reported by a connector (Gmail adapter, part D).
    75	
    76	    Attributes:
    77	        source_id: Stable external key (e.g. `gws_mail_<msgId>`), used as
    78	            the `MailSeed.mail_id` when applied.
    79	        subject: Mail subject, already truncated to 80 chars by the connector.
    80	        body: Mail body, already truncated to `GWS_MAIL_BODY_CHARS` chars.
    81	        received_at: ISO8601 timestamp the source reports.
    82	    """
    83	
    84	    source_id: str
    85	    subject: str
    86	    body: str
    87	    received_at: str = ""
    88	
    89	
    90	@dataclass
    91	class SyncSummary:
    92	    """Aggregate sync outcome for logs / `scripts/gws_probe.py` output.
    93	
    94	    Counts only — never carries titles, subjects, or bodies (§16.3 failure
    95	    handling: credentials and content must not appear in error text either).
    96	    """
    97	
    98	    tasks: int = 0
    99	    schedules: int = 0
   100	    mails: int = 0
   101	    skipped: int = 0
   102	    errors: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   103	    complete: bool = True
   104	
   105	
   106	@dataclass
   107	class FetchResult:
   108	    """Return value of `SourceConnector.fetch()`.
   109	
   110	    Attributes:
   111	        tasks: Tasks fetched from the source.
   112	        schedules: Schedule reminders fetched from the source.
   113	        mails: Mail seeds fetched from the source (empty unless the Gmail
   114	            adapter is enabled).
   115	        complete: True only if every page of every underlying resource
   116	            (task lists, calendar events, gmail messages) was fetched
   117	            successfully. Callers must only perform destructive
   118	            reconciliation (marking missing `source="gws"` tasks done,
   119	            deleting out-of-window/cancelled `source="gws"` schedules) when
   120	            `complete` is True; otherwise apply upserts only (§16.3
   121	            completeness barrier).
   122	        errors: Human-readable failure descriptions (counts/HTTP status/
   123	            resource name only — never credentials or content).
   124	        cancelled_ids: Calendar event ids the source reports as
   125	            `status == "cancelled"` within the fetched window, for callers
   126	            to reconcile away the corresponding `source="gws"` schedule.
   127	    """
   128	
   129	    tasks: list[TaskRecord] = field(default_factory=list)
   130	    schedules: list[ScheduleRecord] = field(default_factory=list)
   131	    mails: list[MailRecord] = field(default_factory=list)
   132	    complete: bool = True
   133	    errors: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   134	    cancelled_ids: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   135	
   136	
   137	class SourceConnector(abc.ABC):
   138	    """The data-source differentiation point (§16.3).
   139	
   140	    Implementations fetch one owner's external data and return it as a
   141	    `FetchResult`. They must not write to `Store` — reconciliation is the
   142	    caller's responsibility (`apply_fetch_result`, in `secretary.run_sweep`'s
   143	    sync-then-detect step).
   144	    """
   145	
   146	    @abc.abstractmethod
   147	    def fetch(self, owner_employee_id: str, today: str) -> FetchResult:
   148	        """Fetch `owner_employee_id`'s current external data.
   149	
   150	        Args:
   151	            owner_employee_id: The employee whose data to fetch.
   152	            today: Reference date as `YYYY-MM-DD` (from `DEMO_TODAY` / the
   153	                sweep's `demo_today`), used for the Calendar window.
   154	
   155	        Returns:
   156	            A `FetchResult`. On partial failure (e.g. one task list's
   157	            pages fail), still returns whatever was fetched successfully,
   158	            with `complete=False` and the failure reasons appended to
   159	            `errors`.
   160	        """
   161	        raise NotImplementedError
   162	
   163	
   164	def apply_fetch_result(
   165	    store: Any, owner_employee_id: str, result: FetchResult, today: str
   166	) -> SyncSummary:
   167	    """Reconcile a `FetchResult` into `Store` (§16.3). Implemented in C-2.
   168	
   169	    This is deliberately unimplemented in the current step (part C, pure
   170	    connector layer only). It is wired up once `models.py` gains
   171	    `Task.source` / `Task.last_seen_due` / `Schedule.source` and `Store`
   172	    gains identities / deletion / `list_tasks(source=)` (§16.3「モデル拡張」).
   173	
   174	    Intended contract for C-2:
   175	        - Upsert every `TaskRecord` / `ScheduleRecord` / `MailRecord` by its
   176	          `source_id`, writing only the connector-owned fields verbatim.
   177	        - `reschedule_count` increments only when the fetched `due_date`
   178	          differs from the stored `last_seen_due` on a record that already
   179	          existed before this sync (never on first sync — first sync only
   180	          sets `last_seen_due`).
   181	        - `created_at` is set once, at first sync. `status_changed_at` is
   182	          set to the source's `last_updated_at` at first sync, and to the
   183	          sync time whenever `status` changes thereafter.
   184	        - Destructive reconciliation (marking missing `source="gws"` tasks
   185	          `"done"`; deleting out-of-window or `cancelled_ids` `source="gws"`
   186	          schedules) happens only when `result.complete` is True. Otherwise
   187	          only upserts are applied.
   188	        - Mail seeds: append-only (no destructive reconciliation); existing
   189	          `mail_id`s are never re-inserted.
   190	
   191	        Args:
   192	            store: The `Store` implementation to write into.
   193	            owner_employee_id: The employee these records belong to.
   194	            result: The `FetchResult` to reconcile.
   195	            today: Reference date as `YYYY-MM-DD`.
   196	
   197	        Returns:
   198	            A `SyncSummary` describing what was written.
   199	    """
   200	    raise NotImplementedError(
   201	        "apply_fetch_result is implemented in C-2, after models.py gains "
   202	        "Task.source/Task.last_seen_due/Schedule.source and Store gains "
   203	        "identities/deletion/list_tasks(source=) per design.md §16.3"
   204	    )

codex

     1	"""CLI probe for GoogleWorkspaceConnector using the author's own ADC (design.md §16.3, ゴール28).
     2	
     3	Usage (author ADC already logged in with tasks.readonly/calendar.readonly[/gmail.readonly]):
     4	
     5	    PYTHONPATH=src .venv/bin/python scripts/gws_probe.py --owner emp_jordan_lee
     6	
     7	Prints counts, per-source completeness, and error strings only. Titles,
     8	descriptions, subjects, and bodies are never fetched-and-shown here — the
     9	connector's `fetch()` return value already withholds nothing but counts from
    10	this script by construction, but this script additionally never prints the
    11	record contents even though they are present in memory.
    12	
    13	`--apply-to-memory` additionally reconciles the fetch into a throwaway,
    14	empty `InMemoryStore` (via `apply_fetch_result`) and prints the resulting
    15	morning digest's counts by kind/tier only — still no titles or bodies.
    16	"""
    17	
    18	from __future__ import annotations
    19	
    20	import argparse
    21	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    22	
    23	from knowledge_discovery.connectors.base import apply_fetch_result
    24	from knowledge_discovery.connectors.google_workspace import (
    25	    GoogleWorkspaceConnector,
    26	    default_session,
    27	)
    28	
    29	
    30	def main() -> int:
    31	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    32	    parser.add_argument("--owner", required=True, help="Probe target's employee_id")
    33	    parser.add_argument(
    34	        "--today",
    35	        default=None,
    36	        help="Reference date YYYY-MM-DD (default: current UTC date)",
    37	    )
    38	    parser.add_argument(
    39	        "--apply-to-memory",
    40	        action="store_true",
    41	        help="Reconcile the fetch into an empty InMemoryStore and print digest counts by kind (no titles/bodies).",
    42	    )
    43	    args = parser.parse_args()
    44	
    45	    today = args.today or datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()
    46	
    47	    session = default_session()
    48	    connector = GoogleWorkspaceConnector(session)
    49	    result = connector.fetch(args.owner, today)
    50	
    51	    print(f"owner: {args.owner}")
    52	    print(f"today: {today}")
    53	    print(f"tasks: {len(result.tasks)}")
    54	    print(f"schedules: {len(result.schedules)} (cancelled: {len(result.cancelled_ids)})")
    55	    print(f"mails: {len(result.mails)}")
    56	    print(f"complete: {result.complete}")
    57	    print(f"errors: {len(result.errors)}")
    58	    for error in result.errors:
    59	        print(f"  - {error}")
    60	
    61	    if args.apply_to_memory:
    62	        from knowledge_discovery.store import InMemoryStore
    63	
    64	        store = InMemoryStore()
    65	        summary = apply_fetch_result(store, args.owner, result, today)
    66	        kinds: dict[str, int] = {}
    67	        for schedule in store.list_schedules(owner_employee_id=args.owner):
    68	            kinds[schedule.kind] = kinds.get(schedule.kind, 0) + 1
    69	        statuses: dict[str, int] = {}
    70	        for task in store.list_tasks(owner_employee_id=args.owner):
    71	            statuses[task.status] = statuses.get(task.status, 0) + 1
    72	        print("--- applied to empty InMemoryStore ---")
    73	        print(f"synced tasks: {summary.tasks} by status: {statuses}")
    74	        print(f"synced schedules: {summary.schedules} by kind: {kinds}")
    75	        print(f"synced mails: {summary.mails} (skipped: {summary.skipped})")
    76	    return 0
    77	
    78	
    79	if __name__ == "__main__":
    80	    raise SystemExit(main())

 def build_fixed_personas() -> tuple[list[Agent], list[Profile]]:
     """Build the 4 fixed agents and their corresponding profiles."""
     agents: list[Agent] = []
     profiles: list[Profile] = []
 
     for item in FIXED_PERSONAS_DATA:

             agent_id=item["agent_id"],
             employee_id=item["employee_id"],
             display_name=item["display_name"],

             endpoint=item["endpoint"],
             active=True,

                 visibility=pi["visibility"],
                 reviewed=pi["reviewed"],

             employee_id=item["employee_id"],
             name=item["display_name"],

 def populate_store(
     store: Store,
     embedder: Embedder | None = None,
     dry_run: bool = False,
     today: str | None = None,
 ) -> tuple[int, int]:
     """Populate store with seed agents, profiles, and Milestone 3 secretary records.
 
     Returns:
         (agent_count, profile_count)
     """
     agents, profiles = generate_all_seeds(embedder=embedder)

         print(f"Total M3 Tasks to store:    {len(m3_tasks)}")

         print("\n--- Sample Profiles (First 5) ---")
         for p in profiles[:5]:
             priv_keys = [it.key for it in p.items if it.visibility == "private"]
             priv_info = f" [Private items: {priv_keys}]" if priv_keys else ""
             print(f"  • {p.employee_id}: {p.name} - {p.role}{priv_info}")
             for it in p.items:
                 print(f"      [{it.key}] ({it.visibility}): {it.body[:65]}...")
         return len(agents), len(profiles)

     for a in agents:
         store.save_agent(a)

     for p in profiles:
         store.save_profile(p)

     for s in m3_schedules:
         store.save_schedule(s)

 def main() -> None:
     """CLI entrypoint for seed generation script."""
     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Generate and load seed data for knowledge-discovery.")
     parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Print summary without writing to Firestore.")
     parser.add_argument("--use-firestore", action="store_true", help="Write to live Firestore instance.")
     parser.add_argument("--project", type=str, default=None, help="Google Cloud project ID.")
     parser.add_argument("--database", type=str, default=None, help="Firestore database ID.")
     parser.add_argument(
         "--today",
         type=str,
         default=None,
         help="Base date for relative seed generation (YYYY-MM-DD). Defaults to DEMO_TODAY or today.",
     )
     parser.add_argument(
         "--clear",
         action="store_true",
         help="Delete all collections (agents/profiles/messages/tasks/schedules/mail_seeds/cards) "
         "before seeding. Use for a demo reset (requires --use-firestore).",
     )
     parser.add_argument(
         "--embedder",
         choices=["deterministic", "gemini"],
         default="deterministic",
         help="Embedder used for stored profile embeddings. Use 'gemini' when the server "
         "runs with GeminiEmbedder (embedding dimensions must match at query time).",
     )

   350	def generate_synthetic_profiles(count: int = 396) -> list[Profile]:
   351	    """Generate 396 high quality synthetic employee profiles deterministically."""
   352	    profiles: list[Profile] = []
   353	
   354	    # Distribution counts
   355	    staffing_count = 158
   356	    real_estate_count = 59
   357	    transition_count = 40
   358	    corporate_count = 99
   359	    exec_count = count - (staffing_count + real_estate_count + transition_count + corporate_count)  # 40
   360	
   361	    dept_assignments: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = (
   362	        [("staffing", STAFFING_ROLES)] * staffing_count
   363	        + [("real_estate", REAL_ESTATE_ROLES)] * real_estate_count
   364	        + [("transition", TRANSITION_ROLES)] * transition_count
   365	        + [("corporate", CORPORATE_ROLES)] * corporate_count
   366	        + [("executive", EXECUTIVE_ROLES)] * exec_count
   367	    )
   368	
   369	    dept_local_counters: dict[str, int] = {}
   370	    for idx, (dept, role_list) in enumerate(dept_assignments, start=1):
   371	        emp_id = f"emp_synth_{idx:03d}"
   372	        f_name = FIRST_NAMES[(idx * 7) % len(FIRST_NAMES)]
   373	        l_name = LAST_NAMES[(idx * 13) % len(LAST_NAMES)]
   374	        full_name = f"{f_name} {l_name}"
   375	        role = role_list[idx % len(role_list)]
   376	        # E-3: overlap branches must key on the department-LOCAL index — the
   377	        # global idx never satisfies `<= 5` for departments that start at 159+
   378	        local_idx = dept_local_counters.get(dept, 0) + 1
   379	        dept_local_counters[dept] = local_idx
   380	
   381	        # Intentional overlap design:
   382	        # First 5 in real_estate: strong overlap with Demo 1 (medical clinic relocation & zoning)
   383	        # First 5 in transition: strong overlap with Demo 2 (practice retirement & succession)
   384	        if dept == "real_estate" and local_idx <= 5:
   385	            current_work = (
   386	                f"Coordinates site acquisition and municipal zoning clearances and permits for outpatient medical facilities. "
   387	                f"Evaluates parking ratio compliance, ADA access, and plumbing infrastructure conversions for clinic tenants. "
   388	                f"Assists regional health networks with complex clinic relocation planning."
   389	            )
   390	            expertise = "Medical zoning codes, retail-to-clinic adaptive reuse, healthcare facility tenant representation."

     1	"""CLI probe for GoogleWorkspaceConnector using the author's own ADC (design.md §16.3, ゴール28).
     2	
     3	Usage (author ADC already logged in with tasks.readonly/calendar.readonly[/gmail.readonly]):
     4	
     5	    PYTHONPATH=src .venv/bin/python scripts/gws_probe.py --owner emp_jordan_lee
     6	
     7	Prints counts, per-source completeness, and error strings only. Titles,
     8	descriptions, subjects, and bodies are never fetched-and-shown here — the
     9	connector's `fetch()` return value already withholds nothing but counts from
    10	this script by construction, but this script additionally never prints the
    11	record contents even though they are present in memory.
    12	"""
    13	
    14	from __future__ import annotations
    15	
    16	import argparse
    17	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    18	
    19	from knowledge_discovery.connectors.google_workspace import (
    20	    GoogleWorkspaceConnector,
    21	    default_session,
    22	)
    23	
    24	
    25	def main() -> int:
    26	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    27	    parser.add_argument("--owner", required=True, help="Probe target's employee_id")
    28	    parser.add_argument(
    29	        "--today",
    30	        default=None,
    31	        help="Reference date YYYY-MM-DD (default: current UTC date)",
    32	    )
    33	    args = parser.parse_args()
    34	
    35	    today = args.today or datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()
    36	
    37	    session = default_session()
    38	    connector = GoogleWorkspaceConnector(session)
    39	    result = connector.fetch(args.owner, today)
    40	
    41	    print(f"owner: {args.owner}")
    42	    print(f"today: {today}")
    43	    print(f"tasks: {len(result.tasks)}")
    44	    print(f"schedules: {len(result.schedules)} (cancelled: {len(result.cancelled_ids)})")
    45	    print(f"mails: {len(result.mails)}")
    46	    print(f"complete: {result.complete}")
    47	    print(f"errors: {len(result.errors)}")
    48	    for error in result.errors:
    49	        print(f"  - {error}")
    50	    return 0
    51	
    52	
    53	if __name__ == "__main__":
    54	    raise SystemExit(main())

  `GWS_SELF_EMPLOYEE_ID` 未設定時、全agent/profile所有者に同期する一方、実コネクタはownerを使わず常に `@me`／`primary` を取得する。同じ作者データを最大400人・複数テナントへ複製し得る。§16が実証対象とするのは作者ADC＋単独所有者であり、未実装のマルチユーザー同期を一般化した範囲超過。
